# Languages (I18N)

OpenVireo is in English, since Front ES-REL (2026-11-09) in Spanish (es-419) and since Front PT-REL (2026-11-10) in Brazilian
Portuguese (pt-BR): a browser whose first language is any `es-*` opens Spanish, any `pt` or `pt-*` Brazilian Portuguese, every
other browser English, and the English / Español / Português (Brasil) selector changes it (user decisions of 2026-11-09, AGENTS.md;
plans `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-es.md` and `front-i18n-pt-br.md` in the research root; the Spanish release is the ES-REL
section and the Portuguese release the last section of this file).
This file describes the infrastructure of Front ES0, the shell slice of Front ES1, the observatory of Front ES2, the exercise
fronts ES3–ES9, the help files (ES-HELP), the chunk split (ES-SPLIT) and the release (ES-REL), in the order they were built.
The status lines of each front describe the tree when it merged. Until ES-REL Spanish was hidden (decision 5): only a
`?lang=es-419` preview link showed it, and from ES1 that preview had the whole shell in Spanish (header, menu, home, toolbar, tour, Help menu web text, project links, error and recovery
texts, instructor mode, `document.title`) and a language selector. From ES2 the optical observatory was Spanish too, from ES3 the whole E01 flow, and so on front by front until every screen was.

## What a translation is

- Translations are **web adaptations by OpenVireo**. They never claim to be CLEA's.
- CLEA's original bytes, hashes, manifests and provenance do not change.
- **Files the site writes stay English** and byte-identical in every language (decision 3). This covers TXT, FITS, CSV,
  HTL, PLR, saved investigations, sessions, options files and every other export.
- **Text on screen that mirrors a written file** (a native list window, a results preview) stays English, with a caption in
  the screen language (decision 9, F2 below).
- **Numbers keep the decimal point** on screen in every language, as typed inputs and files do (decision 4).
- The Spanish addresses the student as **tú** (decision 6).

## The language of one load

The language is settled once per page load, in `src/boot.ts`, which `src/main.tsx` imports first. `index.html` and the entry
are unchanged. Boot calls `settleLanguage` (`src/i18n/settle.ts`, unit-tested with a stand-in page):

1. **Decide** (`src/i18n/locale.ts`, `decideLocale`, a pure function):
   1. a `?lang=` link parameter: `en` or `es-419` (`qps` only in the dev server). It applies to that load and is not stored;
   2. the stored choice: localStorage `openvireo-language`, read in a try/catch;
   3. the browser language: the first entry of `navigator.languages` decides; `es`, `es-MX`, `es-ES`… give es-419, and
      everything else gives English.
2. **Release gate (decision 5).** Until ES-REL, `SPANISH_RELEASED` was `false`: steps 2 and 3 were skipped, so only an explicit
   `?lang=es-419` preview link asked for Spanish. Since ES-REL it is `true`.
3. **Bundle.** English needs none, and the dev-only pseudo-locale is built synchronously from the English sources. Spanish is
   its own chunk, `src/i18n/es/index.ts`, imported dynamically only for a Spanish load (see "How the Spanish bundle arrives").
4. **View original (English)** (decision 9): when localStorage `openvireo-vireo-originals` is `1`, the bundle's VIREO
   originals are dropped, so `vireo()` returns VIREO's exact texts while OpenVireo's own text stays translated. Read at load.
5. **Activate** it (`activateLocale`), set `<html lang>`, and, for a non-English load, `document.title`. A bundle that cannot
   be built or does not validate (a wrong catalog, an unknown map id) is logged and replaced by English, so a broken
   translation never blanks the page.

**No text is read when a module evaluates.** The production build evaluates the shared chunks that the entry chunk imports
*before* the entry chunk's own code (Rolldown), and a Spanish load evaluates every app module before its chunk is active. So
no module may read text when it evaluates:
- every `pickMessages` map, and the catalog-backed `CLUSTER_NATIVE_HR_MESSAGES`, is a `lazyMessages` view
  (`src/i18n/registry.ts`) built on first read. Since ES1 a read of a nested part or a function (`const M=X.entry`,
  `const f=X.format`) returns a view of that part or a function that builds when called, so module-level aliases stay lazy;
- module-level translators are made on their first call (`lazyTranslator`);
- module-level tables of text are built on first read (`lazyMessages(()=>({…}))`) or read through getters. ES1 converted the
  last ones: the target catalog labels and groups in `OpenVireo.tsx`, `SESSION_HINTS` and `SESSION_NOTES` in
  `OpticalOptionsDialog.tsx`, the Sky and Star exposure session notices, the reference titles in `SpectrumInstrument.tsx`,
  the Radio Save texts and translators in `RadioReceiverWindows.tsx` and `RadioControlPanel.tsx`, and two Object X aliases;
- `t` and `vireo()` read the active language when they are called.

`src/i18n/moduleEvaluation.test.ts` imports every source module with no language settled and fails on any text read.

**Guard.** In the dev server and in Vitest, asking for text before the language is settled throws (the tripwire). In
production the request is answered in English and counted (`earlyTextRequests`), and boot logs a console error with the
count; the active-language holder is set only by `activateLocale` (and, since ES-SPLIT, by `extendLocale` and
`markAreaFailed` for a route area's text). `tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts` opens every route of the production build and
a `?lang=es-419` preview, and requires that the holder is activated once, then extended once per route area loaded (same
language), never read while empty, and that no console error appears. The holder lives on `globalThis` (`Symbol.for('openvireo.i18n.active')`), so a
re-evaluated copy of the module (a hot update, `vi.resetModules`) keeps the language.

`src/boot.ts` statically imports only the catalog machinery (`src/i18n/catalog.ts`, `active.ts`, `locale.ts`, `maps.ts`,
`settle.ts`) and `pseudo.ts`, which the production build drops with its `import.meta.env.DEV` branch. `src/i18n/index.ts`
re-exports them with `t`. Entries that do not go through `main.tsx` import `src/i18n/useEnglish.ts` first: the Vitest setup
(`vite.config.ts`) and the standalone test harnesses under `tests/harness/`.

### How the Spanish bundle arrives (ES0 measurements, ES1 decision)

The plan made `src/boot.ts` the page entry, loaded the Spanish bundle lazily and then ran `await import('./main')`, with a
Vite plugin that preloads the app chunk in `index.html`. That was built and measured, and it changed three things:
- **The window load event** no longer waited for the app: a dynamic import does not delay it. Tests that act right after
  `page.goto()` (for example `focus-after-layout.spec.ts` and `cluster-native-observation-hr.spec.ts`) failed, because
  `goto()` returned before the app had evaluated. A top-level await in the entry does not delay the load event either.
- **Rolldown's chunks** changed: with the app as a dynamic entry, six small shared chunks appeared and five disappeared,
  and the app's CSS came out in another module order (`help.css` became its own file; `hubble-lab.css`,
  `spectrum-engine.css` and `help.css` moved before the base styles). The computed styles of every element were compared on
  twelve routes and six menu and phone states, with no difference, but the order was no longer Vite's own.
- **Size:** +3.2 KB gzip on the files `index.html` loads (+2.2 KB raw), mostly chunk overhead.

A top-level await in the catalog module, to keep `main.tsx` as the entry, split the entry far more (+13.6 KB gzip). The
reviewer measured `build.rolldownOptions.output.strictExecutionOrder: true` at +22.3 KB gzip and +20.2 KB brotli, with eight
more files.

**ES1 decision: option (c).** Boot stays synchronous. For English (and qps) the language is active before `main.tsx`
renders, exactly as before. For Spanish, `settleLanguage` returns a promise: boot imports `src/i18n/es/index.ts`, activates it
(or English, logging the failure), and `main.tsx` calls `createRoot(...).render(...)` only when that promise settles. Because
no module reads text when it evaluates, no text is read before activation. The chunk races a 10 s timeout
(`LANGUAGE_TIMEOUT_MS`); whichever settles first decides once, so a chunk that arrives later is never activated. When the
chunk fails, times out or does not validate, the page renders in English and shows a one-line notice in both languages
(`LANGUAGE_FAILURE_NOTICE`, `src/i18n/active.ts`: its own small text, since the Spanish catalog is what failed); settle
returns `{language, failed}`. Measured on the production build:
- the release smoke (`tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`) and a preview probe show the language holder set once and never
  read empty, in English and in Spanish;
- English loads never request the Spanish chunk or the language selector;
- the English entry, chunks and stylesheets are those of fab442e except the additions listed under "Loading cost";
- a Spanish load's window `load` event no longer waits for the app's first render (the chunk is awaited after it); the
  English suite is unaffected, and the Spanish specs wait for the page's own heading.

**Loading cost.** Measured with `npm run build` on the files `index.html` loads (gzip and brotli per file):

| | files | raw | gzip | brotli |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| main 033f693 (before ES0) | 77 | 4,005,050 | 1,011,899 | 740,546 |
| fab442e (ES0) | 76 | 4,009,239 | 1,013,702 | 742,340 |
| ES1 (with review 1) | 76 | 4,013,295 (+4,056) | 1,015,201 (+1,499) | 743,592 (+1,252) |

Against fab442e, three files grow: the entry (`index`: the Spanish chunk's import stub with its timeout, the deferred
render, the failure notice, the lazy selector stub and the module-level conversions), the shared chunk that holds the i18n
machinery (lazy views, `lazyTranslator`, partial-map completion, the new map ids) and the main stylesheet (+192 B, the
review-1 header layout fix of C5, which applies to English too). `index.html` lists the same files (one preload moved); every
other CSS file is byte-identical. The Spanish chunk is 22,375 B (7,537 gzip, 6,685 brotli); the selector, loaded only when
shown, is 4,131 B (1,705 gzip) plus its own 492-byte stylesheet.

## The language selector (ES1)

`src/components/LanguageChoice.tsx`, lazily loaded; until Front HEADER (2026-11-10) at the foot of the header menu beside the theme switch, since then split in two from the same module: the `<select>` (`LanguageSelect`, with its reload dialog) in the page bar beside Help, and "View original (English)" (`VireoOriginalsOption`) at the foot of the sidebar under the theme switch. Its texts are the map
`languageChoice` (`src/components/LanguageChoiceMessages.ts`), outside en.json, so they arrive with the selector's own chunk (before
ES-REL only preview loads fetched it; since ES-REL every load does, after the first render).
- **Shown** (`selectorShown`, `src/i18n/locale.ts`): since ES-REL on every load (English loads fetch its small chunk too); before it
  only on a load whose address had `?lang=en` or `?lang=es-419` exactly (the preview), so the reload flow could be tested.
- **English / Español** radio buttons, each named in its own language under the bilingual legend "Idioma / Language".
- **Changing** opens a confirmation in the current language. It says the page reloads on the same page and what that keeps
  and loses, checked against what the app persists:
  - lost: everything only on screen (pointing, running exposures, measurements, open windows, instructor mode, anything not
    saved to a file);
  - kept: files the visitor saved (Restore investigation JSON loads them again), and in this browser the confirmed
    observation results (`openvireo.observation-notebook.v1`) and display preferences (theme, menu sections, the tour, the ?
    key, Demonstration Mode).

  Buttons: Save investigation (the toolbar's save, which downloads the investigation first), Change and reload, Cancel
  (also Escape). Before ES-REL the choice was **never stored** (user decision in ES1 review 1, C2): the preview language came
  only from `?lang=`, so a tester's choice could not start applying after the release. Since ES-REL the choice is stored
  (try/catch) and the page reloads at the same address; when storage fails, or the address already has `?lang=`, the choice is
  carried as `?lang=` too, and a failed store is said in the dialog (it is not remembered). The #route is kept.
- **View original (English)**: a checkbox stored in `openvireo-vireo-originals`, applied at the next load (decision 9). In
  ES1 it changes only the vireo() sites already translated (the Help menu captions and Results Editors); more come with ES2.

## Three kinds of text

| Source | How it is translated | Where the translation lives (from ES1) |
|---|---|---|
| `src/i18n/en.json` (`t`) | `t` delegates to the active catalog; missing keys fell back to English until ES-REL (since then the catalog is complete; a split module still builds with the fallback) | `src/i18n/es/es.json` |
| The 26 `*Messages.ts` files, `help/tour.ts` and `help/projectLinks.ts` (31 maps) | `export const X=pickMessages('id',X_EN)`, built on first read; the 6 flat translators default to `pickMessages('id',X_EN)` | `src/i18n/es/maps/<id>.ts`, typed `Translation<typeof X_EN>`, or `PartialTranslation` before ES-REL (`BundleMaps`, `src/i18n/maps.ts`) |
| VIREO's own literals in `src/core/` | opt-in at each render site: `vireo(LITERAL)` | `src/i18n/es/originals.json`, keyed by the normalised original |

**`*Messages.ts` maps.** Consumers read the picked name (`CAMERA_MESSAGES`, `CLUSTER_HR`, `OBJECT_X_LAB`, …). The `_EN` name
is the typed English source; tests and the Spanish maps use it. In English, every picked map reads the English map's values.
- Before ES-REL a Spanish map could be partial: `resolveMessages` checked it with `mapParity(…,{partial:true})` and completed the
  missing strings with English (`completeWithEnglish`, `src/i18n/parity.ts`), as the catalog's fallback mode does. Since ES-REL a
  Spanish map must be complete (a partial one stays English and is logged in production; `es.test.ts` fails on it).
- The tour's text and the project links' text are maps since ES1 (`TOUR_EN`, `PROJECT_LINKS_EN`); the tour's targets and the
  English issue template (`ISSUE_TEMPLATE`, F5) are not text and stay outside them.
- `src/i18n/maps.ts` lists the 31 ids (`MAP_IDS`) and types a bundle's maps by id (`BundleMaps`). `activateLocale` refuses a
  bundle with an unknown map id.
- On first read, `resolveMessages` checks the bundle's map against the English map with `mapParity`
  (`src/i18n/parity.ts`): the same keys at every depth, the same kind, the same `{param}` set and the same function arity.
  A mismatch throws in the dev server and tests; in production that map stays English and the problem is logged.
- Two maps are **catalog-backed** and therefore not registered: `CLUSTER_NATIVE_HR_MESSAGES` (every value is a `t()` call,
  built lazily) and `RADIO_MESSAGES_EN` (its translator renders through `t`). Their Spanish comes with en.json, so coverage
  does not count them twice and qps marks them once.
- Each id is picked in exactly one source line (`infrastructure.test.ts`).
- Known limit: the dev pseudo-locale also marks map leaves that are not display text, if a map holds any; such a leaf would
  show up as a marked value in a file or a failed lookup, and is moved out of the map when found.

**`vireo()` (`src/i18n/vireo.ts`).** Core never imports the catalog (`scienceBoundary.test.ts`), and its literals stay
byte-exact. A screen site wraps the literal; file writers and the F2 mirrors never do. So the same constant can feed a
translated dialog and an English file.
- The dictionary key is the normalised original: single `&` accelerators removed, `&&` kept as `&`, trimmed.
- `vireo(text,'ctx')` separates one English text that needs two translations (key `ctx|text`).
- `scripts/audit-ui-messages.mjs` `auditOriginals` lists every tsx site that renders, outside `vireo()`, a member of:
  - an UPPER_CASE import from `src/core/`;
  - a `*_LITERALS`, `*_CAPTIONS` or `*_NAMES` import from any module (VIREO literals kept beside web text, for example
    `TERMINATE_SLEW_LITERALS`, `RESULTS_EDITORS_LITERALS`, `HOT_LIST_POPUP_LITERALS`, `PRINT_SKY_LITERALS`,
    `HELP_BUTTON_NAMES`);
  - a local alias of either (`const D=SOME_DFM`). (Until ES4 also `const C=m.native`, the Wedge map's VIREO captions; ES4 moved
    them out of the picked map, see its section.)

  It also lists the multi-word literals of the `.ts` presentation modules (`webHelp.ts`, `tour.ts`, `multiSpecText.ts`).
  Each needs a receipt in `src/i18n/vireo-literals.json` (`src/i18n/vireoAudit.test.ts`).
- The baseline receipts every unwrapped site as `pending-translation`. After ES1 (which wraps the Help menu captions and the
  Results Editors names, and moves the tour into a map): 757 sites in 65 files and 112 module literals (webHelp.ts),
  658 receipts.
  Each sub-front wraps its area's sites, or records why one stays English (`native text mirrored in a file`,
  `identifier`).
- After ES2: 418 receipts, 28 of them `identifier` (the observatory's), 390 still `pending-translation` (562 sites in the
  exercise areas of ES3 onwards).

## Numbers

`formatNumber`, `fixed` and `scientific` use the `en-US` number locale in every UI language (`locale`). Spanish and English
outputs are identical; `infrastructure.test.ts` checks a vector of values. Since Front PT-REL (user decision of 2026-11-10,
AGENTS.md) a language may separate thousands its own way on screen: the registry's `thousands` replaces en-US's group separator in
`formatNumber`'s output, and Brazilian Portuguese sets U+202F («24 000», «2 400 000»). The decimal point, `fixed`, `scientific`, input
syntax and every file the site writes are unchanged; English, Spanish and the pseudo-locales keep the comma. See "Brazilian
Portuguese: the release (PT-REL)".

## Dev-only pseudo-locale (qps)

Open `?lang=qps` in the dev server. Every catalog, map and `vireo()` string is wrapped as `⟦…⟧`, its vowels are accented and
it is padded about 35 % longer (the expected Spanish length); `{params}` are kept. Map functions have their result marked
only, since it may carry data.
- Visible text without `⟦` is text that does not go through the catalog, a map or `vireo()` yet.
- A download that contains `⟦` is presentation text that reached a file.
- `src/boot.ts` uses `src/i18n/pseudo.ts` only in an `import.meta.env.DEV` branch, so the production build drops it;
  `scripts/check-i18n-dist.mjs` (part of `npm run build`) searches every text file under `dist/` (the app, `labs/`, the
  sitemap, the original text resources) and fails on `⟦`, a `\u27e6` escape in any case, an HTML character reference or a
  pseudo-locale chunk.

## Tests

- **English suite.** Playwright pins `locale:'en-US'` in all four configs (`playwright.config.ts`, `.cloud`, `.pages`, `.scripts`); Vitest activates English in its setup file. Since ES-REL the Spanish specs set `locale:'es-MX'` themselves (`tests/fixtures/language.ts`), and since PT-REL the Portuguese ones `locale:'pt-BR'` (`contextLocale`).
- **Exports identity** (`tests/i18n/exports-identity.spec.ts`). Each family is downloaded in English and in qps from the
  same path; the file names and SHA-256 must be equal and the qps bytes must not contain `⟦`. In the qps run the flow's own
  part of the page must show a `⟦`, so a route where the pseudo-locale did not apply cannot pass by comparing English with
  English. Covered in ES0 (11 tests, 23 files):
  - measurement CSV, velocities CSV, analysis session JSON;
  - Hubble CSV and Hubble session JSON;
  - complete investigation JSON, classification CSV, observation notebook, native TXT (unchanged bytes) and the generated
    native CSV;
  - HTL save;
  - the options file (`VEO_OPTS.json`);
  - variable-star Save Text (As Displayed);
  - the pulsar results CSV;
  - the star spectrum `.SSP` save;
  - the radio receiver's Download capture as `.PLR` (raw export of a model-generated recording);
  - Object X with the identity still hidden: the kept spectrum CSV and the saved investigation (ES9 replaced this family with
    the E08 family, unmasked, in English, qps and Spanish; see its section);
  - the CCD camera FITS and VIREO-header FITS (masked until ES9, which pinned the clock and the random stream; see its
    section).

  ES3 added the galaxy, sky and star exposure CSV and session JSON and the field spectrometer's CSV and session, and runs the
  E01 families in Spanish too. ES4 added the E02 families, ES5 the E03 families and ES9 the E08 families (see their sections). Not yet covered: the native pulsar save, native `.DAT` observations, photometer JSON and the cluster
  TXT. Each exercise sub-front adds its own families, and Spanish joins every family from ES1.
- **Release smoke** (`tests/release/pages-smoke.pages.ts`): every route of the production build, and a `?lang=es-419`
  preview, sets the language holder exactly once and never reads it while empty.
- **Vitest.** `locale.test.ts` (the decision), `infrastructure.test.ts` (map ids, lazy maps, bundle map checks, the
  catalog-backed maps, tripwire, fallback catalog, `vireo()`, qps, numbers and export hashes under qps, `settleLanguage`
  with a broken bundle and with early text requests), `vireoAudit.test.ts` (receipts).
- `createI18n` requires a complete catalog at compile time unless `fallback` is passed explicitly. `activateLocale` stays
  exported for `useEnglish.ts` and the tests; app code never calls it.

## F1: screen text that must not become data

The target picker labels (`sky.supportedStarLabel`, `sky.supportedGalaxyLabel`) and the Object X hidden names
(`OBJECT_X_LAB.hiddenName`, `hiddenRowName`) are screen text. ES0 traced them:
- the labels reach the picker, notices, the motion card and the analyser's library notice (`Library.targetLabel`);
- the hidden names reach the picker, notices, the Object ID boxes and the masked radio hot-list rows.

Data uses the source identity (`id`, `sourceLabel`, the student's `ObjX-<ID>` from `objectXDataLabel`). The export-identity
tests guard the result.

## F2: native views that mirror a file

A `<pre>` whose lines are the bytes a Save writes stays English on screen and in print, with a caption in the screen language
and `lang="en"`. Examples are FieldInfoListWindow, NativeTextResults, the PulsarSavedRows raw text and CameraInformationList.
Their column headers are not translated, which would break the fixed-width layout.

## Spanish (ES1 onward)

- `src/i18n/es/es.json`: the catalog, partial until ES-REL (fallback mode), complete since. ES1 covers the shell: `app.*`, `project.*`,
  `session.*`, `error.*`, `instructorMode.*`, `demonstrationMode.banner`, every `*.nav`, the home and first steps (`sky.eyebrow`,
  `sky.title`, `sky.subtitle`, `sky.about`, `sky.local`, `sky.observatory`, `sky.analyzer`, `sky.step1…3Help`),
  `spectrum.tools` and `classLink.cancel` (130 keys).
- `src/i18n/es/maps/`: `appShell`, `helpMenu`, `languageChoice`, `projectLinks`, `tour` (complete) and partial `objectXLab`,
  `polish`, `variableStars` (their header-menu names).
- `src/i18n/es/originals.json`: VIREO's menu captions shown in the shell (Help, Topics..., Websites, 2MASS Catalog, Project
  CLEA, NVO, About This Exercise..., Results Editors, Observational Results, Pulsar Results).
- `src/i18n/es/glossary.json`: every §5.1 row with a fixed Spanish form (an English pattern requires a Spanish stem, plural
  forms allowed), fixed interface names, kept names (Project CLEA, Gettysburg College, VIREO, CLEA, NSF, GitHub, FITS, CSV,
  VizieR, NOMAD, USNO-B1, GSC, 2MASS, SHA-256, JSON) and forbidden forms. The lint applies to the Spanish strings that exist,
  so rows for areas still in English wait for their sub-front. Forbidden words are matched with Unicode word boundaries
  (JavaScript's `\b` is ASCII-only): Spain-only words, usted and its imperatives (haga, elija, pulse, presione, seleccione,
  abra, guarde, escriba, revise, espere, use, cargue; «su trabajo», «su investigación»), voseo (tenés, podés, querés,
  hacé, elegí, apuntá, abrí, guardá, mirá, usá) and the unaccented verb «pulsar» in any form.
- `src/i18n/es/lint-allowed.json` (ES1 review 2): a forbidden word that is correct in one string, with the reason, for that
  string only. Several usted imperatives (use, guarde, cargue, abra, espere, revise, escriba) are also third-person
  subjunctives, and «vale» appears in «vale la pena»; translators record the exception instead of rewording good Spanish.
  Every allowance must still be needed by its string.
- The phone menu button and the header nav's accessible name read «Menú» (both are "Tools" in English; «Herramientas» does
  not fit beside the brand and Help at 320 px). The analyzer's own sidebar nav keeps `spectrum.tools`.
- **VIREO names in the shell's prose** (until ES2 fixes the menu glossary): Spanish first, with VIREO's English name in
  parentheses at its first mention, as in «Modo de demostración (Demonstration Mode)», «Ejecutar ejercicio (Run Exercise)»,
  «Iniciar sesión (Log In)», «Aceptar (OK)». VIREO menu captions shown as captions go through `vireo()` and
  `originals.json`; "Project CLEA" is an official name and stays English.
- `src/i18n/es/identical.json`: the Spanish strings that may equal their English source, each with a reason.
- **Checks** (`src/i18n/es/es.test.ts`, failing on violations): the catalog is a valid partial catalog; every map has the
  English shape (complete for the shell maps); originals keys are normalised literals that exist in the source; the shell is
  100 % Spanish; identical text only with a reason; the glossary; the glyphs `› … ↗ ▸ · — – × ✳`; ¿ ? and ¡ ! paired both
  ways (a closer without an opener and an opener without a closer both fail); every placeholder a map function prints in
  English also printed in Spanish; no HTML; no claim to be CLEA's. Length ratios outside 0.7–1.6 are reported, not failed.
  A small self-test checks that the lint does catch usted, voseo, Spain-only words and unpaired marks.
- **Pipeline** (`scripts/i18n-batch.mjs`): `extract --area <file or prefix>` writes the untranslated keys of an area with their
  English, {params}, usage lines and a length hint; an agent fills `es`; `merge` checks keys and {params} and writes es.json
  in en.json's order; then the Vitest checks run and an independent reviewer agent reads the batch in context (decision 7: no
  human sample).
- **End to end**: `tests/i18n/es-shell.spec.ts` (the chunk, `<html lang>`, the title, an English-versus-Spanish comparison of
  every shell text, the Help menu, the tour, instructor mode, the recovery notice and 320 px) and
  `tests/i18n/language-selector.spec.ts` (the gate, Cancel and Escape, Save, Change and reload in both directions, blocked
  storage, View original). `exports-identity.spec.ts` runs the toolbar's Save investigation in English, qps and Spanish.
- **Dist** (`scripts/check-i18n-dist.mjs`): the Spanish catalog's text, and the Spanish text of the maps and originals,
  appears in exactly one chunk, which `index.html` neither loads nor preloads.
  Since ES2 review 1 it also has a size budget for that chunk: a warning above 150 KB gzip and a failure above 250 KB
  gzip (ES2: about 55 KB gzip; ES5: 118.3 KB). The chunk is not split now; ES5 measured first-render times on throttled
  connections and recommends a per-route-area split before ES-REL (see its section). A minified string may be written as "…", '…' or `…`,
  and the check looks for all three forms.
- **Header layout** (ES1 review 1, C5; a small English CSS change): in the wide-screen sidebar the top row wraps, so Help
  moves under the brand instead of overlapping it when they do not fit (with the CI's DejaVu fonts, in English too), the
  status line wraps instead of being cut off, and the theme options may shrink to stay inside their pill. The pinned row can
  therefore grow (80 px before; 134 px with DejaVu in either language), so the sidebar's `scroll-padding-top` is 11rem: the
  wrapped worst case plus one menu item, so keyboard focus moving up the sidebar is never hidden under it (WCAG 2.4.11;
  ES1 review 2). `es-shell.spec.ts` presses Shift+Tab from the bottom of the menu at 1440 px in both languages and fails if
  a focused item's top is under the pinned row.
  `es-shell.spec.ts` checks overlap, clipping and the pills at 1440 px in both languages and at 320 px in Spanish.

## Spanish: the observatory (ES2)

ES2 translates the optical observatory route (`#observatory`) and fixes the VIREO menu glossary.

- **Catalog:** 546 keys (about 8,000 English words) of the observatory and its instruments as they show on that route: `sky`, `optical`, `motion`,
  `horizon`, `siteClock`, `runningClock`, `scopeAccess`, `instrumentAccess`, `instrumentParameters`, `cameraParameters`,
  `cameraInfo`, `cameraPrepare`, `palette`, `testMode`, `demonstrationMode`, `optionsFile`, `photometerTab`, `htl`,
  `multispec`, `notes.summary`, and the guide and lab keys that the observatory files render (`pleiadesLab`, `classLab`,
  `ph`, `spectrum`… where OpticalTelescope, HotListEditor or the instrument cards use them). Two new keys:
  `nativeMirror.caption` and `nativeMirror.captionPrint` (F2), and `sky.targetCoordinates` (it replaced a hard-coded
  "RA … h · Dec …°").
- **Maps, complete:** `observatory`, `fieldInfo`, `fieldReadDetails`, `nakedEye`, `qsoList`, `sound`, `observationMetadata`,
  `polish` and `camera`. The Help buttons' accessible names moved from `HELP_BUTTON_NAMES` into the `helpMenu` map
  (`buttonNames`), because they are web text.
- **VIREO literals:** 372 new originals in `originals.json` (382 in all) (menus, DFM captions, MessageBox texts, the options dialog's
  option names and hints, the Site, Date/Time, Sky Coordinates, Hot List, Catalog Object, QSO List, Field Information,
  camera parameter and Multi-Spec captions). Every observatory render site goes through `vireo()`, `vireoList()` (a list of
  captions rendered with `.map`) or `vireo(menuCaptionText(…))`. A Spanish string that is identical to VIREO's (GCVS, QSO,
  J2000.0, General, No…) is left out of `originals.json`; `vireo()` returns the literal.
- **Texts the core composes around data** (a name, a file name, coordinates) take a translator: `hotListEntryAddedText`,
  `variableAddedText`, `variableNewListText`, `opticalHotListCaption`, `opticalHotListSavedText`, `radioHotListMissText`,
  `opticalSlewConfirmText`, `opticalSlewBelowText`, `hotListPrintHeader`, `printSkyFooter`, `siteInformationLines` and the
  Multi-Spec `removeQuestion` have a last parameter `tr` (default: the literal itself, so English and every file are
  unchanged); the screen passes `vireo`. `vireo()` keeps a literal's own leading and trailing spaces around the translation
  ('RA:  ' before coordinates). The slew-limit box is keyed whole (`manualLimitText`, four keys). A piece that cannot carry
  its own ¿ ? pair (the Multi-Spec remove question) is keyed with the `fragment` context (`fragment|…`), and the ¿ ¡ pairing
  check skips those keys.
- **Prints (F3):** the hot-list print header ("Laboratory Table Number (Not Recorded)"), the Print Sky Image footer
  ("Coordinates of Field Center:", "Right Ascension:", "Field Size:", "Instrument:"…), the Site Information list and its
  print, and the Field Information print header follow the screen language. "VIREO - The VIRtual Educational Observatory"
  stays as VIREO's product line.
- **F2 mirrors on the observatory:** the Field Information lists (star list, variables, QSO list; their lines are what Save
  Text writes) and the camera information lines keep `lang="en"` and, in a non-English load only,
  `<NativeMirrorCaption>` (`src/components/NativeMirrorCaption.tsx`): "Texto nativo de VIREO, en inglés como en el archivo
  que guarda." (or "…como lo muestra VIREO." for a list that is printed, not saved). The window captions around them
  are translated.

### The VIREO menu glossary (final)

`glossary.json` `names` now holds one Spanish form for every menu, dialog caption and button of the observatory tree, and
later fronts reuse them. Main forms: File › Archivo, Slew › Mover, Tools › Herramientas, Help › Ayuda, Set Coordinates ›
Fijar coordenadas, Hot List › lista de objetos, View/Select from List › Ver/seleccionar de la lista, Add Current
Item/Position to List › Agregar el objeto o la posición actual a la lista, Slew to Selection › Mover a la selección, Field
Information › Información del campo, Variable Stars › Estrellas variables, QSO List › Lista de QSO, Print Sky Image ›
Imprimir imagen del cielo, HST Image Archive › Archivo de imágenes del HST, Exit Observatory › Salir del observatorio, Set
Date/Time › Fijar fecha/hora, Site Parameters › Parámetros del sitio, Request Time › Solicitar tiempo, Optical Simulation
Options › Opciones de simulación óptica, Limit Slewing › Limitar movimiento, Restrict Scope Access › Restringir acceso a
telescopios, Show Object ID › Mostrar ID del objeto, Edit Mode › Modo de edición, Virtual Observatory › Observatorio
virtual, CCD Camera › Cámara CCD, Multi-Channel Spectrometer › Espectrómetro multicanal, OK › Aceptar, Cancel › Cancelar,
Close › Cerrar.
- A name marked `exact` (a single generic word: OK, File, Close, Help, Options…) applies only when the whole English string,
  accelerators removed, equals it; other names apply wherever the English contains them. Spanish case follows the sentence
  (the check ignores case). A name may list `except` ids with a reason (the header's own "Tools" menu reads «Menú», ES1).
- **In prose**, a VIREO name is written in Spanish with VIREO's English in parentheses at its first mention, for example
  «Limitar movimiento (Limit Slewing)». In a menu path the English follows each segment, «Mover (Slew) ▸ Fijar coordenadas
  (Set Coordinates)», so the path glyphs keep their count.
- The term check ignores `{param}` names (`{count}` is not "counts") and identical strings listed in `identical.json`;
  "daylight-saving" is not "save".
- **ES2 review 1 decisions** (final): pointing as a noun is «posición del telescopio» in prose (short labels: «Posición actual»,
  «Centro de la posición»); «apuntado» as a noun is forbidden by a glossary pattern that leaves the participle alone
  («el telescopio está apuntado»). "Supported" is «compatible». Multi-Spec Init › «Preparar», Go › «Comenzar», its list's
  Display › «Ver»; Terminate Current Slew? › «¿Detener el movimiento actual?»; Observation List › «Lista de observaciones»;
  Save Updates › «Guardar cambios»; Compute Rdshft Vel. › «Calcular vel. por corrimiento al rojo»; Allow User Change ›
  «Permitir cambios del usuario»; Dark Noise › «Ruido de corriente oscura»; Chip Well Depth › «Capacidad de pozo del
  chip»; the Sky Coordinates dialog › «Coordenadas del cielo para mover el telescopio».
- **No nested parentheses:** when VIREO's English gloss would fall inside parentheses, it goes in square brackets
  («(… en las Opciones de simulación óptica [Optical Simulation Options])»), or it is dropped after its first mention in
  that string.
- **View original also covers the web buttons that carry a VIREO name** (review 1, C1): the Slew and Site items, Optical
  Simulation Options, File, Exit Observatory, the list window's Search and Print, the Find dialog and the box buttons
  (OK, Cancel, Yes, No) render `vireo()` or `vireoMenuItem()` (VIREO's '...' becomes the web '…') of
  `OBSERVATORY_VIREO_CAPTIONS` or the core literals, not a web map entry. Set Date/Time and Site Information are the web's
  short names of VIREO items [W]. The Multi-Spec Information and print lines take `tr` too (they are shown and printed,
  never saved).

### Checks added by ES2

- `src/i18n/es/es.test.ts`: the nine maps are complete; originals keys are read with a small tokenizer (escapes such as
  `\r\n` decoded, quotes inside other literals handled) plus the composed slew-limit texts; map functions are sampled with
  string arguments, then with an argument that is both a number and «An», then with numbers.
- `tests/i18n/es-observatory.spec.ts`: one walk through the observatory (Slew, Site, File, Tools, Exit, the student options
  box, then in instructor mode the options dialog, Reset, Edit Mode, Virtual Observatory, Field Information and the QSO
  List) in English, Spanish and qps. Spanish may not repeat an English text except identifiers; qps must mark every text;
  the F2 caption and `lang="en"`; View original (English) at the next load; and the layout of every step at 320 px and
  1440 px (no page overflow, nothing wider than its dialog, no clipped control, no dialog or menu outside the viewport),
  to be run with the DejaVu fontconfig.
- `tests/i18n/exports-identity.spec.ts`: the HTL save, the options file (`VEO_OPTS.json`), the CCD camera FITS and
  VIREO-header FITS, and a new family, File › Field Information (Star Data List Save Text and Comma Delimit) with the QSO
  List Save Text, also run in Spanish and must match English byte for byte (with the documented masks). A Spanish run also
  fails on any Spanish letter (á é í ó ú ñ ¿ ¡) in the compared bytes.
- The audit (`vireo-literals.json`): every observatory site is wrapped or carries an `identifier` receipt (numeric input
  lengths, download file names, telescope and galaxy names, pixel offsets, the star target id). `vireoList` counts as a
  wrapper.
- **Not translated here:** the exit reminder (VIREO's Reminder.obs, a help file: ES-HELP); the spectrometer, Hubble and
  other exercise routes (ES3 onwards).

## Spanish: exercise E01, Hubble's law (ES3)

ES3 translates the E01 flow: the Hubble lab guide, the galaxy, sky and star exposures, the spectrometer route and the
single-slit spectrometer at a free pointing, the spectrum analyser (z step, the engine's Zoom, Record measurement and VIREO's
Observational Data, the measurement log, the Observational Results table of your confirmed redshifts, velocity analysis) and the
Hubble diagram.

- **Catalog:** 632 keys (about 8,400 English words): `hubble.*`, `hubblePoint.*`, `lab.*`, `spectrum.*`, `galaxyExposure.*`,
  `skyExposure.*`, `starExposure.*`, `velocity.*`, `fieldSpec.*`, `ssp.*`, `spectrometer.*`, `skyProfiles.*`, `exposureIO.*`,
  `drift.*`, `ownResults.*`, and the `continuity.*`, `classLink.*`, `classImport.*`, `lss.*` and `ph.*` keys these screens
  render. Two new keys with the same English as before: `spectrum.observationTypeError` (the web Sp.Type refusal [W], which the
  core keeps as `OBSERVATIONAL_SP_ERROR`) and `hubble.source.typed` (`velocitySource` now also projects "Saved measurement N ·
  typed in Observational Data").
- **Maps, complete:** `engineView` and `spectralRange`. VIREO's own engine captions (Zoom, Set Wavelength Range, its two
  slider captions, Reset, OK, Cancel, the Reset hint, Cursor, Wavelength:, Rel. Intensity:) moved out of the picked map into
  `ENGINE_VIEW_LITERALS` (`src/SpectrumEngineMessages.ts`), so their render sites use `vireo()` and View original applies.
- **VIREO literals:** 27 new originals (430 in all): the Spectral Range dialog and its warning (`SPECTRAL_RANGE_LITERALS`), the
  engine captions, the Observational Data dialog (`OBSERVATIONAL_DATA_LITERALS`: caption, field captions, the photometry box
  legends, OK, Cancel), its velocity boxes (`LSS_DIALOG_TEXT`, shown in the dialog's refusal line) and the spectrometer
  readout (Object:, App. Mag (V):, Telescope, Sky). Wrapped sites: `SpectralRangeDialog`, `SpectralRangeRow`, the star route's
  Spectral Range button, `SpectrumEngineControls`, `ObservationalDataDialog` (shared with the cluster and photometer routes),
  `SpectrometerReadout`, and the M31 notice on the field spectrometer. Identifier receipts: the band letters of Observational
  Data (V: B: U: R: I:), `HUBBLE_RECIPES` (ids and numbers), `HUBBLE_SOURCES` (the source documents as recorded),
  `HUBBLE_TARGETS` (Hubble.HTL ids and labels) and `STAR_TARGET.id`. After ES3: 392 receipts, 38 `identifier`, 354
  `pending-translation` (523 sites).
- **Kept as recorded (data, not translated):** the galaxy line names of the original `GLXYLNS.LST` (K Line (Ca II), H Line
  (Ca II + H), G Band (Blend)), the Hubble.HTL galaxy labels, the workbook's row names and cell references, and the source
  document titles of `HUBBLE_SOURCES`.
- **Files:** the measurement CSV, velocities CSV, analysis session, Hubble CSV and session, `.SSP`, the galaxy, sky and star
  counts CSV and session JSON, and the field spectrometer's counts CSV and exposure session are byte-identical in English,
  qps and Spanish (`exports-identity.spec.ts`).
- **Layout (one English-visible change):** at 570 px and below the analyser plot's rotated "Display level" label was 2 px
  outside the plot's left edge with DejaVu fonts, in English too; it now has `dominant-baseline:central` (class
  `axis-label-y`, `src/style.css`) at those widths only. Wider screens are unchanged.
- **Checks:** `tests/i18n/es-e01.spec.ts` walks the whole flow in English, Spanish and qps (guide with every disclosure open,
  galaxy exposure, analyser, Zoom, Observational Data, Hubble diagram from the workbook example and from the recorded
  measurement, sky and star exposures with the `.SSP` panel, the Spectral Range warning and dialog, the spectrometer route, and
  the single-slit spectrometer at NGC 4874). Spanish may repeat an English text, option, accessible name, title or placeholder
  only when it is in an exact list of identifiers (galaxy labels, ids, line names, formulas, units) or is a plain number
  with its unit, a hash or a workbook cell reference; qps must mark every other text. It also checks View original
  (English) at load, the English captions, and the layout at 320 px and 1440 px (page overflow, dialogs, clipped or crossing
  controls, plot text outside its SVG), to be run with the DejaVu fontconfig.
- **ES3 review fixes:**
  - VIREO's Display/Print Data caption on the confirmed-redshifts table is `OWN_RESULTS_LITERALS.displayData`
    (`src/core/ownObservationResults.ts`) through `vireo()`; the catalog key `ownResults.sharedShow` is gone.
  - The M31 notice's adaptation line is OpenVireo's text, so it left `M31_NOTICE` for the catalog key `sky.m31Adaptation`
    (observatory and field spectrometer); VIREO's caption and message stay under `vireo()`.
  - `src/i18n/es/vireoSites.test.ts` resolves every `vireo()`, `vireoMenuItem()` and `vireoList()` argument that is a string or a
    member of a constant imported from a `.ts` module (through local aliases too), and requires a Spanish original or an
    `original:` reason in `identical.json`, so web text wrapped in `vireo()` by mistake fails. Arguments built at run time are
    left to the screen specs.
  - One name per VIREO box: App. Mag (V): › «Mag. aparente (V):», written «Mag. aparente (V) [App. Mag (V)]» in prose;
    Observation Time (JD) › «Fecha de observación (JD)»; Sp.Type in prose › «el tipo espectral (Tipo esp.)».
  - Terms: random draws › «extracciones aleatorias» (also in the ES2 strings that had «sorteo»); the exposure's committed time
    and counts › «acumulado» (ES2's motion and clock strings keep «confirmado», where it means an applied position); the Reset
    counts button's prose › «Reiniciar cuentas»/«reiniciada»; single-slit › «de una rendija».
  - VIREO's own message boxes quoted in a reason stay in English inside “…” with a Spanish gloss in [ ]; the ¿ ¡ pairing
    check skips an English quote that the English source contains.
- **Not translated here:** VIREO's Observational Results window (Display/Print Data) and the other Results Editors, which
  belong to a later front; the help files (ES-HELP).

## Spanish: exercise E02, Large-Scale Structure (ES4)

ES4 translates the E02 flow: the LSS guide, the LSS survey (field list, row statuses, pointing, the exposure, the measurement
panel, Record Z and VIREO's Measured Redshift Data, the results, Save Data, the description), the wedge (`#lss-wedge`: the panel,
its Options dialog, the Load Saved Data prompts and notices, the selected record, Print Wedge Plot), and the instructor's LSS
Galaxy Data (Edit Mode). The multi-object spectrometer, which a student may reach at an LSS-strip pointing, was already
complete (ES2); it was read in context at NGC 4889 (Coma – 2) and needed no change.

- **Catalog:** 167 keys (about 2,300 English words): `lss.*` (132), `lssLab.*` (31) and `wedge.*` (4). Sixteen English keys left
  en.json because they were VIREO captions rendered from web keys (the rule of ES3): `lss.dialog.title`, `.jdTime`, `.raGroup`,
  `.decGroup`, `.zLabel`, `.velocity`, `.ok`, `.cancel`, `.object` (review), `lss.region.yes`, `.no`, `.cancel`, `.okBox`, `lss.fieldsError.ok`,
  `lss.measure.record` and `lss.results.txt`. Their sites now render `vireo()` of VIREO's text, with the same English on screen.
  The dialog's Object and Remarks labels are VIREO's "Object:" and "Remarks:" (`LSS_DIALOG_CAPTIONS`) through `vireo()`, without
  the colon, so the English still reads Object and Remarks; the results table's Remarks header keeps its web key
  (`lss.dialog.remarks`).
- **Map, complete:** `wedge` (`src/i18n/es/maps/wedge.ts`). VIREO's own wedge captions left the picked map: `WEDGE_MESSAGES_EN`
  (type `WedgeWebMessages`) is web text only, and `wedgeMessages()` adds `native`, built per call by `wedgeNativeCaptions()`
  through `vireo()`/`vireoList()` (so View original applies). The three messages VIREO composes around a file name are split into
  `WEDGE_NATIVE_PIECES` (core) and translated piece by piece with the `fragment` context. The panel's `messages` overrides (the
  test harness) still apply on top. The audit no longer lists `const C=m.native` sites.
- **VIREO literals:** 55 new originals (486 in all): the wedge window, menu, Options dialog (`pointStyle|Open`/`Filled`, a
  context so the point style does not collide with a menu's Open), its load messages and prompts, the drawn "RA (Hours)" and
  "Points Plotted: " (the axis titles "Z(=V/C)" and "V(km/sec)" stay as symbols), Print Wedge Plot...; LSS Galaxy Data's dialog,
  boxes and Search Results lines (`lssRegionSummary` takes `tr`); Measured Redshift Data's DFM captions (`LSS_DIALOG_CAPTIONS`,
  `src/core/lssRedshiftDialog.ts`, from the research DFM dossier), the Z Measurement Saved box, Save Data, Record Z, the Save
  dialog title ("Save Reshift Measurements in Text File", VIREO's spelling) and the LSSFields.LST "File Error!" box
  (`lssFieldsFileErrorText` takes `tr`). One name per VIREO caption: Measured Redshift Data › «Datos de corrimiento al rojo
  medidos», Record Z › «Registrar Z», Save Data › «Guardar datos», Plot Data › «Graficar datos», LSS Wedge Plot › «Diagrama de
  cuña LSS», LSS Galaxy Data... › «Datos de galaxias LSS...», Load Saved Data › «Cargar datos guardados».
- **Terms:** survey › «sondeo», wedge › «cuña»/«diagrama de cuña», to plot › «graficar», journal › «diario» (as ES3).
  Remarks › «comentarios» everywhere (review S1; «observaciones» is ambiguous in an observatory): VIREO's "Remarks:" original
  (Observational Data and Measured Redshift Data) is «Comentarios:», `htl.remarks` «Comentarios HTL», and the ES2/ES3 strings
  that named remarks say «comentarios»; the glossary has a `remarks` row. Tab characters › «tabulaciones»: the glossary's
  `tabs` row (a browser tab › «pestaña») skips "tabs, line breaks / or other / BOM" and "line breaks and tabs", which a second
  row maps to «tabulaci…», so a mistranslated browser tab still fails.
- **English-visible change (review N3):** `lss.zSavedWeb` named a menu that does not exist ("LSS wedge plot is in the Tools
  menu"); it now gives the real path, "the LSS Wedge Plot opens from Tools › Analysis › LSS data" (Spanish «Menú › Análisis ›
  Datos LSS»).
- **Receipts:** the LSS and wedge sites are wrapped; the rest are `identifier` (colour schemes, guide step ids and counts, input
  lengths, a field's record count). After ES4: 372 receipts, 47 `identifier`, 325 `pending-translation`. `(.TXT)` after Save
  Data is a scientific-literal receipt. Three new `vireo()` sites take a value built at run time (the Measured Redshift Data
  refusal's caption and message, the LSS Galaxy Data input error), checked by `es-e02.spec.ts`: `UNRESOLVED_SITES` is 106.
- **Kept as recorded (data, not translated):** galaxy ids, the LSSFields.LST items in the field selector (only the «(N galaxias)»
  around them is Spanish), LSS_FieldNN.HTL, telescope names, the GLXYLNS.LST line labels on the measurement plot, the wedge's
  receipt name "OpenVireo LSS records", saved .TXT lines, hashes and binary32 bit patterns, and the file header words that the
  wedge table quotes ("MJD(Days)", "V(km/sec)"). Spanish identical to English (identical.json): Observable, JD, z, Dec (°) and the
  wedge's symbols and units.
- **Files:** `exports-identity.spec.ts` adds one E02 family test, run in English, qps and Spanish: the LSS counts CSV and exposure
  session, the `.SSP`, the results CSV, `LSS_Redshifts.TXT`, the saved investigation (with the survey and its description), the
  wedge points CSV before and after Load Saved Data, and `LSS_Galaxy_Data.TXT`. The page clock is paused at a fixed instant
  before the exposure and only moves by `runFor` until the investigation is saved; nothing is masked (the record JD and the
  region rows' JD are the observatory clock's, which stands still). Proven stable with `--repeat-each=10 --workers=4`.
- **Checks:** `tests/i18n/es-e02.spec.ts` walks the flow in English, Spanish and qps (26 steps, with both Measured Redshift Data
  refusals and both LSS Galaxy Data input errors). Spanish may repeat an English
  text only when it is in an exact identifier list or matches a narrow pattern (galaxy ids, hashes, bit patterns, the en-US print
  date, saved .TXT lines, numbers with units); qps must mark every other text, `<option>` texts and SVG titles included. Every
  VIREO caption of the walk (the dialogs, prompts, notices, boxes, Options and the drawn wedge text) is asserted explicitly in
  Spanish, in English, and in English under View original (English), where the web text around it stays Spanish; the
  LSSFields.LST "File Error!" box is checked both ways with the file blocked. Layout at 320 px and 1440 px with the DejaVu
  fontconfig: no page overflow, dialogs inside the viewport, no clipped or crossing control, wedge text inside its SVG.
- **Not translated here:** the help files (ES-HELP), VIREO's Observational Results window (a later front).

## Spanish: exercise E03, Spectral Classification (ES5)

ES5 translates the E03 flow on `#classification`: the classification guide, the lab (classification source, the original
program list with its dialog and a local `.LST` import, atlas and standard pairs, Standard − practice, the line table, the
wavelength window, the range dialog, the four renderings with their reverse options, the normalized sampled difference, the
comparison cursor and its readout, the sampled flux readout, the manual equivalent width with its cursor, comparison marks,
review dialog and records, the classification review and records, an imported spectrum and an acquired star) and the three
cards that render only on this route: the observation notebook (`ObservationResults`, with its edit, delete and recovery
dialogs), the saved observation DAT inspector (`NativeObservationResults`, with its record editor, delete and results-list
tools) and the saved results TXT/CSV inspector (`NativeTextResults`). The plan's ES5 list did not name those three cards; they
were brought into scope because no other route shows them.

- **Catalog:** 732 keys (about 10,700 English words): `class`, `classLab` (the 26 left by ES2/ES3), `classLink`, `classImport`,
  `classAtlas`, `classLines`, `program`, `programImport`, `range`, `viewport`, `feature`, `cursor`, `widthSource`,
  `widthCoordinates`, `comparisonMark`, `paneSource`, `panMarks`, `sharedMarks`, `combinedWidth`, `probe`, `sampledDiff`,
  `sampledTrace`, `photo`, `combined`, `inspection`, `reverseTrace`, and the cards' `obs`, `nativeResults`, `nativeEdit`,
  `nativeDelete`, `nativeList`, `nativeText`, `nativeCsv` (the last `nativeResults` key, `nativeResults.number.infinity`, is
  shared with the cluster HR tools). Every E03 file and prefix is at 100 %. The prefixes `palette` and `drift` were already
  complete (ES2/ES3).
- **Keys that left en.json** because they rendered VIREO captions (the rule of ES3): `program.open` ("Program List"),
  `program.next` ("Next on List") and `obs.dialogTitle` ("Observational data"); `program.end` keeps only its web sentence.
- **VIREO literals:** 5 new originals (491 in all):
  - `CLASSIFY_PROGRAM_LITERALS` (`src/core/classificationProgram.ts`): TSpClassify's DFM items "Program &List..." and
    "&Next on List" [D] (research `analysis/claude-audit-2026-10-26/veo-forms.json`), shown as the web's buttons without VIREO's
    "...", and the end box's "No More Spectra" and "You are at the end of the spectra list." (literals at 0x50b7d4 and 0x50b7e4,
    `docs/CLASSIFICATION-PROGRAM-LIST-PROVENANCE.json`), followed by the web sentence `program.end`. One name per caption: Program
    List... › «Lista de programa...», Next on List › «Siguiente en la lista», No More Spectra › «No hay más espectros».
  - `SPECTRAL_LINE_TABLE_LITERALS` (`src/help/spectralLines.ts`): "Spectral Line &Table" (DFM SpectralLineTable1) on the lab's
    button › «Tabla de líneas espectrales».
  - The observation notebook's dialog heading is VIREO's "Observational Data" (`OBSERVATIONAL_DATA_LITERALS.caption`, already
    «Datos de observación» since ES3) through `vireo()`.

  - the observation notebook's dialog heading reads "Observational Data" (VIREO's caption) instead of the web's "Observational
    data";
  - at 700 px and below, the manual-width cursor plot's view box has a 4-unit margin on each side (`-4 0 488 360` instead of
    `0 0 480 360`): with DejaVu fonts its doubled y-axis label "0.5" and its last tick were cut by 2 px at the plot's edges, in
    English too. Wider screens are unchanged.
- **Receipts:** the 12 E03 receipts are `identifier` (the practice stars of CLASSIFY.LST, the atlas labels of the original atlas
  index, the SPECLNS.LNL lines and their wavelengths, the dataset and line-list SHA-256, counts and step ids). After ES5: 372
  receipts, 59 `identifier`, 313 `pending-translation`. `UNRESOLVED_SITES` stays 106 (the new sites resolve).
- **Glossary:** new `terms` rows standard › «estándar», shoulder › «hombro», grayscale › «grises», notebook › «cuaderno»,
  luminosity › «luminosidad», atlas › «atlas»; new `names` Program List, Next on List, No More Spectra, Spectral Line Table. Other
  terms used in one form throughout: practice › «práctica», unknown › «desconocido», feature › «rasgo», trace › «traza», range ›
  «rango», window › «ventana», zoom in/out › «acercar/alejar», pan › «desplazar», readout › «lectura», fallback › «respaldo», pane
  › «panel», replay › «reproducción», owned (bytes the inspector keeps) › «retenido», Observation Time (JD) › «Fecha de
  observación (JD)» (also `obs.time`).
- **Lint:** the ¿ ? pairing check accepts a closer right after a `{param}` («¿Restaurar los bytes originales de {file}?»),
  with a self-test both ways.
- **Kept as recorded (data, not translated):** star names (HD, BD, HZ, Feige, SAO, O 1015), spectral types of the standards, the
  atlas labels ("Main Sequence", "Luminosity near O8"…), the SPECLNS.LNL line labels ("Ca II (K line)", "H I (H Delta)"…), file
  names (A0000021.SP, CLASSIFY.LST, a student's .LST aliases), the acquired packet's designation "N0000.DAT@14428", hashes, the
  stored text and byte dumps of an inspected DAT record, and the lines of an inspected results TXT. Spanish identical to English
  (identical.json): placeholder-only strings ("{wavelength} Å · {label}", "{position} · {object}"…), SHA-256, and "Single · 32
  bits"/"Double · 64 bits".
- **Not translated here:** the Spectral Line Table window itself (`src/help/SpectralLineTable.tsx`: its list caption, loading,
  error, Try again, Close and its SPECLNS help) stays English until ES-HELP; so do the help files behind the lab's Help button.
  "100 %" above counts en.json keys only (maps and VIREO originals are counted separately by `i18n-coverage.mjs`).
- **ES5 review fixes:**
  - The notebook dialog's field captions are VIREO's (TObsRsltsDlg1): Observation Time (JD), Right Ascension:, Declination:,
    Observed Magnitudes, Redshift (Z):, Velocity (V): now render `vireo()` of `OBSERVATIONAL_DATA_LITERALS`, so View original
    applies; `time`, `ra`, `dec`, `photometry`, `redshift` and `velocity` left the `observationMetadata` map (English, Spanish and
    type; ObservationResults was their only user). The Spanish map had «Hora de observación (JD)»; VIREO's caption is «Fecha de
    observación (JD)», and the notebook's prose says «fecha de observación» too.
  - The web notebook card is «Resultados del cuaderno» (Abrir resultados del cuaderno, Resultados del cuaderno · N), distinct from
    VIREO's list «Resultados de observación (Observational Results)», whose menu path in `obs.reuse` is «Menú › Editores de
    resultados (Results Editors) › Resultados de observación (Observational Results)».
  - One form each: standard pair › «par de estándares», endpoint grid › «rejilla de extremos», raw › «en bruto» (also in the ES2
    observatory map and ES3 strings that had «sin procesar»), merge › «fusión» (also `ownResults.sharedHelp`), Original practice
    library › «Biblioteca de prácticas originales»; glossary `terms` rows for the first four. Wording: «por tu cuenta», «copia
    de seguridad», «sesgo del entorno de ejecución», «Deshacer la última edición/eliminación de observación», «Intensidad
    izquierda/derecha del continuo», «La diferencia numérica (Number de JavaScript)», «Se leen registros…», «vista previa del
    cursor», and the article on every Deshacer button.
  - `tests/i18n/classifyProgramLiterals.ts` holds the restated `CLASSIFY_PROGRAM_LITERALS` that `es-e03.spec.ts` uses;
    `src/core/classificationProgram.test.ts` checks they are equal. `es-e03.spec.ts` gains a step that opens the notebook on the
    imported spectrum and asserts the six field captions and the dialog heading in Spanish, English and View original.
- **Files:** `exports-identity.spec.ts` replaces the ES0 classification family (en and qps) with one E03 family run in English,
  qps and Spanish: the classification session JSON, classification CSV, complete investigation (before and after an import, with
  a width record and an acquisition), observation notebook before and after an edit, the edit recovery, the unchanged and the
  edited DAT, the unchanged TXT, the generated native CSV and the imported spectrum. The page clock is installed at a fixed
  instant, the route loads while it runs, then it is paused before the acquisition's one-second exposure and only `runFor` moves
  it, so nothing is masked; downloads are paced (`tests/fixtures/download-pacing.ts`), since headless Chromium drops the 11th of
  more than ten fast downloads. Proven stable with `--repeat-each=10 --workers=4`.
- **Checks:** `tests/i18n/es-e03.spec.ts` walks the flow in English, Spanish and qps (41 steps). Spanish may repeat an English
  text only when it is an exact identifier or matches a narrow pattern (star names, standard pairs, program-list rows,
  line-table rows whose label is a SPECLNS.LNL label, hashes, original file names, numbers with units); qps must mark every other
  text, `<option>` texts, accessible names and SVG titles included. The VIREO captions of the walk (Program List, Next on List, the
  No More Spectra box, Spectral Line Table, Observational Data and its six field captions) are asserted explicitly in Spanish, in English, and in English under
  View original (English), where the web text around them stays Spanish. Layout at 320 px and 1440 px with the DejaVu
  fontconfig.

### The Spanish chunk and the per-area split (ES5 measurement)

The plan left the per-area split of the Spanish chunk to ES5. Measured on the ES5 production build (`npm run build`).

**Method.** `vite preview` serves `dist/` without compression, so the files were served from loopback (127.0.0.1:5175, and
6310 for the projected build) by a small static server that gzips text at level 6, as a CDN would, with `Cache-Control:
no-cache`. Chromium (Playwright's headless shell) loads each page in a fresh context with the cache disabled and CDP
`Network.emulateNetworkConditions`: **Slow 4G** 1.6 Mbps down, 750 kbps up, 150 ms latency; **Fast 3G** 1.44 Mbps down,
675 kbps up, 562.5 ms latency (DevTools' former preset). The tour is marked done. *First render* is `performance.now()` when an
init-script MutationObserver first sees the app's own content: a header menu button for the home page (`/`; the prerendered
`index.html` already has an `<h1>`, so the heading cannot be used) and `.classification-main h1` for the lab route
(`#classification`, a lazy route). The Spanish chunk's time is its Resource Timing `duration`; JS and CSS are the summed
`transferSize` of the `.js`/`.css` resources finished before first render. Five runs each; medians (the five runs were within
about 1 % of each other). Spanish loads are `?lang=es-419`; every Spanish run rendered in Spanish (no fallback to English).

**Sizes.** 118.3 KB is the chunk compressed by `check-i18n-dist.mjs` (Node zlib, gzip level 9); 118.8 KB in the table is the
Resource Timing `transferSize` from the measuring server (gzip level 6, 118,461 B of body, plus the response headers).

**Projection to 100 %.** The chunk is 118.3 KB gzip (409 KB raw) at 61.4 % of the catalog's keys (60.7 % of its English bytes),
half of the VIREO originals and 19 of 31 maps (the two largest English maps, Pulsar and RadioAntenna, about 106 KB of source,
are not started). Scaling each part (catalog 85 KB gzip ÷ 0.607, originals 10 KB ÷ 0.507, maps 27.5 KB plus the unstarted
166 KB of English map source at the same ratio) gives about 240 KB gzip, before the help files (ES-HELP). A throwaway local copy
of `dist/` (not committed) had the Spanish chunk padded with an appended string array of shuffled Spanish catalog words to
244.6 KB gzip (763 KB raw).

| Profile | Route | English | Spanish now (118.8 KB) | Spanish at 100 % (244.9 KB) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Slow 4G | home | 5.88 s | 6.62 s (+0.74) | 7.25 s (+1.38) |
| Slow 4G | lab | 7.63 s | 8.34 s (+0.71) | 8.98 s (+1.35) |
| Fast 3G | home | 9.90 s | 11.12 s (+1.22) | 11.82 s (+1.92) |
| Fast 3G | lab | 12.17 s | 13.33 s (+1.17) | 14.04 s (+1.86) |

- Spanish chunk transfer: Slow 4G 0.75 s now, 1.38 s at 100 %; Fast 3G 1.22 s now, 1.92 s at 100 %. It starts only when the
  entry chunk has run (5.8 s on Slow 4G, 9.8 s on Fast 3G), so its whole transfer lies on the critical path of a Spanish first
  render; the cost is close to its size divided by the throughput, plus one round trip.
- JS before first render, home: English 1,022,589 B; Spanish 1,141,350 B now, 1,267,450 B at 100 %. Lab: English 1,324,851 B
  (Slow 4G); Spanish 1,413,729 B now, 1,556,218 B at 100 %. CSS: 21,743 B home in both languages; lab 26,509 B English and
  27,058 B Spanish (the language selector's stylesheet).
- The 10 s language timeout (`LANGUAGE_TIMEOUT_MS`) starts when the entry runs; at 100 % the chunk still arrives about 2 s
  after it on Fast 3G, so the timeout is not at risk on these profiles (it would be on links slower than about 200 kbps).
- English is unchanged by ES5: the files `index.html` loads are 4,016,587 B raw, 1,016,514 B gzip (main 1d4bcf6: 4,016,703 and
  1,016,559); the classification chunk grows 385 B raw (226 gzip) for its `vireo()` sites.

**Recommendation: split per route area, not now, before ES-REL.** At today's size a Spanish first render costs about 0.7 s
(Slow 4G) to 1.2 s (Fast 3G) more than English, roughly 12 % on top of a 6–10 s English load that is itself dominated by the
1 MB entry; splitting would save at most that. At 100 % the cost doubles (about +1.4 s and +1.9 s, 19–23 %), and the chunk would
be near the 250 KB failure budget of `check-i18n-dist.mjs` before the help files. The home page needs only the shell, the
observatory and the shared keys, about 27 % of the catalog's English bytes (the `common` area plus the shell and observatory
files) and the observatory maps and originals, so a boot chunk of about 70–80 KB gzip would bring the home page's Spanish cost
back to about +0.5 s on Slow 4G (75 KB × 8 / 1.6 Mbps + 150 ms). Proposed shape, for its own sub-front once the chunk passes the 150 KB warning (expected with
ES6 or ES7):
- `scripts/i18n-areas.mjs` already assigns every key to the files that render it; a build step writes one Spanish module per
  lazy route area (classification, cluster, pulsar/radio, LSS, Object X, photometer, variable stars, help) and a core module
  with the shell, observatory and every key used by more than one area. Maps and originals follow the area of their render sites.
- Boot keeps loading only the core module (unchanged flow, the same 10 s timeout and English fallback).
- Each lazy route's loader fetches its area module in parallel with its own JS chunk (`lazy(()=>Promise.all([import(route),
  loadArea(id)]))`), so a lab adds no extra round trip; English resolves the area immediately and downloads nothing new.
- `check-i18n-dist.mjs` checks every area module (no Spanish elsewhere, none loaded by `index.html`) and budgets each one.

**Status (ES-SPLIT, 2026-11-09):** done, with route areas declared at each route's own lazy site and a core module of about
104 KB gzip instead of 70–80 KB; see "Spanish: the chunk split by route area (ES-SPLIT)" at the end of this file.

## Spanish: exercise E04, Pleiades (ES6)

ES6 translates the E04 flow: the Pleiades guide, the photometer route (`#photometer`: KPNO 0.9 m and Mayall profiles, the U, B, V, R
and I filters, the aperture dialog, sky and target readings, the integration series, the notebook, VIREO's Record/Review with its
message boxes, B−V, Save and Restore photometer session, the photometer at a free pointing read from the GSC tiles, the Mayall domain
explanation and the plot settings) and the Pleiades colour–magnitude analysis. The pointing card and the exercise list were already
Spanish (ES2). The cluster model-fit section that the photometer route also renders (`NativeClusterAnalysis`, `nc.*`/`db.*`/`ce.*`)
and VIREO's Recorded Results window behind Display/Print Data (`CLUSTER_HR`) stay English until ES7 and the Results Editors front;
`nc.error`, which the photometer shows as its own alert, is translated here.

- **Catalog:** 288 keys (about 4,800 English words): `ph.*` (227, now 250/250), `pa.*` (35), the rest of `pleiadesLab.*` (29, now
  67/67) and `nc.error`. Four keys left en.json because they rendered VIREO captions from web keys (the rule of ES3):
  `ph.skyReplaceYes`, `ph.skyReplaceNo.button`, `ph.nativeOk` (the boxes' Yes, No and OK) and `ph.nativeResults` (VIREO's
  Display/Print Data, now `OWN_RESULTS_LITERALS.displayData`). Their sites render `vireo()` with the same English on screen.
- **VIREO literals:** 12 new originals (503 in all after the merge with ES5): Record/Review (`menuCaptionText(vireo(RL.menu))`), the Telescope has Moved!,
  Data Not Saved! and Data Saved boxes (`PHOTOMETER_RECORD_LITERALS`), the Reading Sky! question (`PHOTOMETER_SKY_REPLACE`) and the
  IDText word Unknown. The "Telescope has Moved!" text that VIREO composes around the reading name is split into
  `PHOTOMETER_RECORD_PIECES` (core; `movedText` still returns the same three lines) and translated with the `fragment` context.
  `photometerIdText` (core) takes `tr` for VIREO's Unknown and Sky; the star name stays as recorded. Object:, Telescope, Sky, Yes, No,
  OK and Display/Print Data already had originals. One name per VIREO caption: Record/Review › «Registrar/Revisar» (as ES3's
  prose), Telescope has Moved! › «¡El telescopio se movió!», Reading Sky! › «¡Lectura de cielo!», Data Saved › «Datos guardados»,
  Data from Observations (VIREO's HR view, named in the guide) › «Datos a partir de observaciones» (review:
  «Datos de observaciones» was too close to Observational Data › «Datos de observación»).
- **Receipts:** the ten wrapped sites left `vireo-literals.json`; the other photometer, analysis and guide sites are `identifier`
  (band letters, durations and repeats as numbers, aperture bounds, the PLEIADES.HTL rows, labels, file name, size and hashes, the
  printed standards, the tiled target prefix). After ES6 (with ES5 merged): 362 receipts, 77 `identifier`, 285 `pending-translation`.
  `UNRESOLVED_SITES` is unchanged (106): every new `vireo()` argument resolves statically.
- **Glossary (new rows):** aperture › «apertura» (aperture size › «tamaño de apertura»), notebook › «cuaderno», readings ›
  «lectura», seeded › «semilla» («sesión con semilla»), callback › «llamada» (as ES3), zero point › «punto cero», photons ›
  «fotón/fotones», colour–magnitude › «color–magnitud», cluster fit › «ajuste (manual) del cúmulo»; names Data from Observations ›
  «Datos a partir de observaciones» and Record/Review › «Registrar/Revisar». Other fixed choices: fresh sky › «cielo nuevo», telescope context ›
  «contexto del telescopio», integration series › «serie de integración» (the `integration` row needs «integración»), committed ›
  «acumulado», raw counts › «cuentas en bruto», Sky/Object modes › «Cielo (Sky)»/«Objeto (Object)», ly › «años luz», "port" ›
  «versión web», the compact Guide Star Catalog › «catálogo de estrellas guía (Guide Star Catalog) compacto».
- **Kept as recorded (data, not translated):** the PLEIADES.HTL labels (01799-01442, N2230-01627) and compact GSC names, catalogue
  record ids (N2230.DAT@144496), telescope names, the band letters, the printed spectral labels of the 14 standards (including the
  manual's «BO», «AO»), the PLEIADES.HTL file line with its size and SHA-256, UNKNOWN and ObjX labels, the bibliographic title of the
  2008 manual (glossed in brackets). identical.json: `ph.fixed`, `ph.seriesSecondsOption`, `pleiadesLab.sourceFile`.
- **Files:** `exports-identity.spec.ts` adds one E04 family test, run in English, qps and Spanish: the photometer session JSON
  (`openvireo-pleiades-photometer.json`), the Observational Results list's Save Text and Comma Delimit after two Record/Review OKs,
  and the saved investigation. The page clock is paused before activation and moves only by `runFor`; nothing is masked (the records'
  JD is the observatory clock's). Proven stable with `--repeat-each=10 --workers=4`.
- **Checks:** `tests/i18n/es-e04.spec.ts` walks the flow in English, Spanish and qps (25 steps: the Mayall domain explanation, the
  exercise list and pointing card, the guide with every disclosure open, activation, sky V and B with a running series, the star in V
  and B, the notebook entry dialog, Observational Data, Data Saved, Telescope has Moved!, Data Not Saved!, B−V for two stars, an
  invalid shift, the preview, the confirmed fit with its tables, the aperture dialog's input error, the free-pointing input error,
  Save photometer session, a free pointing read from the GSC tiles, Reading Sky! and its No). Spanish may repeat an English text,
  option, accessible name, title or placeholder only when it is in an exact identifier list or matches a narrow pattern (star labels,
  record ids, the observatory picker's galaxies, hashes, printed spectral labels, numbers with units); qps must mark every other text,
  `<option>` texts and SVG titles included; the target selector's 24 options must read «… · entrada N de la lista de objetos
  original». Every VIREO caption of the walk is asserted explicitly in Spanish, in English, and in English under View original
  (English), where the web text around it stays Spanish. Not walked on screen: the IDText word Unknown (Show Object ID off); `es.test.ts`
  checks its original against the source literal and `currentObject.test.ts` checks that `photometerIdText` passes it through `tr`. Layout at 320 px and 1440 px with the DejaVu fontconfig: no page
  overflow, dialogs inside the viewport, no clipped or crossing control.
- **Translation process (model experiment):** three translator agents on Sonnet each took about 95 strings with a brief (style,
  glossary, fixed UI names, examples); an independent Opus reviewer read all 288 strings and the originals in context. Their raw
  output and the fixes are summarised in the ES6 report to the coordinator.
- **ES6 review fixes:** Data from Observations › «Datos a partir de observaciones» everywhere (glossary name row too); "Enter" a
  value › «Escribe» (as earlier fronts), "completed reading" › «lectura completada», "expectations" › «valores esperados»,
  "committed" random state › «acumulado», Save investigation named as such in `pa.preview`/`pa.unconfirmed`, and Tabla 2/3 in the
  standards' source line. `photometerNativeRecord.test.ts` checks that the pieces rebuild `movedText` byte for byte.
- **Not translated here:** the cluster model-fit section on the photometer route and the Data from Observations view (ES7), VIREO's
  Recorded Results window (Results Editors front), the photometer help list (ES-HELP), the browser's own file-input text.

## Tools

- `node scripts/i18n-areas.mjs`: which files render each en.json key (static `t('key')`, then prefix rules for keys built at
  run time).
- `node scripts/i18n-coverage.mjs [--areas]`: per area (the file that renders each key, or common), per key prefix, per map
  and for the originals, how much has Spanish.
- `node scripts/i18n-batch.mjs extract|merge`: the translation batch pipeline (see Spanish above).

## Spanish: exercise E05, cluster HR and variable stars (ES7)

ES7 translates the E05 flow: on `#cluster-database` the cluster guide, the reference-database lab (dataset choice, point
inspection and exclusion, Possible Cluster Member with its removal question and Add to Hot List, Select Cluster Data with its
print preview and its no-selection box, Display/Print Data with its saves, the native ZAMS/isochrone trial and confirmation, the
comparison table, View/Print Parameters and its print preview, the HR print preview), the own-observation HR panel (Data from
Observations, `ownHr`), the saved-observation HR tool (`obsHr`: file review, plot, Observational Data, overlays, the Recorded
Results list), the help viewer's VIREO captions (THelpLstDlg/THelpWin), the variable-star route (`#variable-stars`: search,
Variable Star Data, its saves and print preview, Simulated Light Curve and its print preview) and File › Cluster Data… on
`#observatory` (`ClusterDataWindow`: the dialog, the Clusters for Study list, the field report and their boxes). The pieces the
plan's ES7 line did not name were brought in because no other front renders them: the Pleiades page also shows
`NativeClusterAnalysis` (its `nc` and `pa` keys), and the Cluster Data window was left out of the ES2 observatory walk.

- **Catalog:** 295 keys new on main (about 3,800 English words) in `db`, `ce`, `ownHr`, `obsHr`, `clusterLab`, `nc`, `pa`; every E05 file
  and prefix is at 100 %. Three keys left en.json because they rendered VIREO's own menu captions (THRDisplayForm1 DFM):
  `obsHr.plotHeading` (Load/Plot), `obsHr.reportHeading` and `db.reportSummary` (View/Print Parameters); they now render
  `vireo(CLUSTER_HR_CAPTIONS.loadPlot|viewPrintParameters)`. ES7 also translated the 35 `pa.*` keys and `nc.error`, which
  ES6 translated first on main; ES6's text was kept at the merge (with «gráfica» and «Inspeccionar…» aligned to this
  catalogue in `pa.coverage`, `pa.rows` and `pa.standards`). On the merged tree en.json goes 3178/3570 → 3473/3567 keys
  (89.0 % → 97.4 %).
- **Maps:** `clusterHr` and `clusterData` are new complete Spanish maps (`src/i18n/es/maps/clusterHr.ts`, `clusterData.ts`) and
  `variableStars` is now complete. Maps started on the merged tree: 28/31.
- **VIREO literals moved out of web maps** (the rule of ES3: a VIREO caption never renders from a web key or map):
  - 49 entries left `CLUSTER_HR_EN`. 43 moved to `src/core/clusterHrCaptions.ts` (`CLUSTER_HR_CAPTIONS`: the help viewer's
    captions, menus and boxes, Possible Cluster Member's menu and field captions, Print, Select Cluster Data...,
    Display/Print Data, the list windows' menus, Comma Delimit, Information, Yes/No), with Load/Plot and View/Print Parameters
    added; 6 now render the core literal they duplicated (`memberOpen` › `POSSIBLE_MEMBER_CAPTION`, `obsDataColumn` and

    `hrPrintCaption` › `CLUSTER_HR_PRINT_LITERALS.caption`).

    (moved from `ClusterDataWindow.tsx`).

- **Glossary:** the `names` row Data from Observations › «Datos a partir de observaciones» (also added by ES6). Terms used in one form throughout:
  hot list › «lista de objetos» (also "hotlist"), plot › «gráfica», report › «informe», slider › «control deslizante», trial ›
  «ajuste de prueba»/«prueba», turn-off › «punto de desvío», apparent modulus › «módulo aparente», preset › «valor predefinido»,
  runtime › «entorno de ejecución», floating-point › «punto flotante», ly › «años luz» (prose) and «a. l.» (beside a number),
  log10(age/yr) › «log10(edad/año)», (B−V) adjustment › «Corrección de (B−V)». Coordinator rules: «Escribe» (never
  Introduce/Ingresa), «lectura completada», «valor esperado»; «por tu cuenta», never «tú mismo».
- **Lint:** `lint-allowed.json` allows «use» in `clusterData.useTelescopeOff`, where it is the quoted VIREO caption; drive ›
  «accionamiento» (main's glossary) there too.
- **Not translated here:** the help topic window's heading prefix ("Help - "), its topic text and the not-found text
  (`helpNotFoundText`) stay with ES-HELP, which also edits `src/clusters/CleaHelp.tsx`. The English
  `ownHr.adaptation` names the button "Plot my photometer records", whose label is "Plot my observation records"; the Spanish
  names the real button.
- **Files:** `exports-identity.spec.ts` ends with one E05 family run in English, qps and Spanish: the investigation after a
  confirmed fit with an exclusion, Display/Print Data's Save Text and Comma Delimit (CLEA Cluster Data), the observation DAT
  written back by the HR tool, the Recorded Results list's Save Text and Comma Delimit, File › Cluster Data…'s report Save Text
  (NGC 752) and the two HTL files its hot-list items write (Yes in "Current Hot List not Saved!" downloads the current list,
  then the entire list), and the variable-star Save Text. The Cluster Data report's Comma Delimit makes no file: VIREO answers
  its "not available" box, a row being shorter than the column positions. The Date is fixed (`setFixedTime`) and nothing is
  masked.
- **Checks:** `tests/i18n/es-e05.spec.ts` walks 50 steps (the cluster route, the variable-star route, then File › Cluster Data…
  on `#observatory`, where only the Cluster Data windows and their boxes are compared) in English, Spanish and qps. Spanish may
  repeat English only for the exact identifiers in `SAME` and narrow anchored patterns: cluster names from the contract,
  reference-star IDs, spectral types, the picture text, the print date, GCVS names, the catalog's own variability types (read
  from the page's type list) and the light curve's plot text (variable-star steps only)
  and CLUSTERS.DAT names (Cluster Data steps only). The VIREO captions of each step are asserted explicitly in Spanish, in
  English and in English under View original, where the web text around them stays Spanish; the Cluster Data steps include the
  No Stars Found in Field!, Current Hot List not Saved! and New Hot List Created boxes. Layout at 320 and 1440 px under the
