  `vireo()` and `vireoMenuItem()` has no bidi control, and under View original returns VIREO's literal exactly; `iso`, `plotText`
  and `ltrText` return their input.
- *Screen (Chromium):* `dist/` of main 88576b4 and of this branch served side by side (`vite preview` on loopback) and scanned on the
  home page and the 16 routes, every `<details>` opened, with the clock fixed: every text node (HTML and SVG), `document.title`,
  `<html lang dir>` and the `aria-label`, `title`, `placeholder`, `alt`, `aria-description` and `data-label` attributes, in en-US,
  es-MX and pt-BR browsers: 51 pages, 85,213 strings, all identical, and no bidi control in any.
- *Build:* the same 2,166 files; besides chunk hashes and the minifier's import aliases, the shared chunk holding the catalog machinery
  grows by 1,072 bytes, the `vireo` chunk by 203 and the entry by 9; `dist/` holds no `qps-rtl` and no Arabic (`check-i18n-dist.mjs`
  passes).
- *Exports and render budget:* `exports-identity.spec.ts` and `render-budget.spec.ts` pass unchanged (counts below).

**Deferred.** `plotText()` at the SVG label sites and the number sites RT7 lists (RT12); `ltrText()` at composed English sites:
the builders that receive `vireo` as a translator (about 40 call sites: hot-list miss text, help and pulsar print pages, LSS region
summary, cluster empty-field text…) isolate each piece, so with View original in a right-to-left load their pieces can be drawn in
reverse order; RT7's walk lists these and RT12 fixes them. (`LssSurvey.tsx`'s label colon strip now sees past a closing PDI, so
an isolated English label reads `Object`, as in English.) Parenthesised English inside a translation ("حفظ (Save As...)") is the translator's text:
its trailing "..." is drawn before "Save"; RT8's lint or a runtime rule for parenthesised Latin groups is RT7/RT8's decision. Mirroring
and islands (RT5), placement (RT6), the walk (RT7), lint (RT8), `dir` on options and notices (RT9), exports under `qps-rtl` (RT10).

**Verification.** `tsc -b`; Vitest `src/i18n`, `src/components` and `src/help` 898 in 39 files (`bidi.test.ts` 10; `pseudoRtl.test.ts`
updated for isolated parameters), `src/theme` and `src/seo` 63; the pin refresh leaves the three pin files unchanged; `npm run build`
with every check. Playwright, Chromium (dev server, DejaVu fontconfig): `tests/i18n` `shell`, `language-selector` and
`exports-identity`, `render-budget` and `scripts-pseudo.smoke` 61; `npm run test:scripts` 85 (the 81 before and the 4 of
`bidi.scripts.ts`). The screen scan above was repeated on the final build.

**Independent review (RT4).** Four findings, fixed: (1) the runtime nested its own output when `t()` or `vireo()` text was passed to
`t()` as a parameter (FSI around LRI); `isolate()` now leaves text that is already one whole isolate alone. (2) A translated format
with no right-to-left letter of its own was forced left to right even when its values were Arabic (an Arabic "{name}: {value}" would
read value first); such a format is now isolated left to right only when its values have no right-to-left letter either, while an
English fallback still always is. (3) `vireo()` and `vireoMenuItem` built an object per call in every load; a left-to-right load now
reads one flag and allocates nothing. (4) `LssSurvey.tsx`'s colon strip (`/:(\u2069?)$/`) sees past a closing PDI; left-to-right
output is unchanged since it never holds one. The reviewer's own checks: `dist/` of main 88576b4 and of this branch, served side by
side, scanned in en-US, es-MX and pt-BR on the 20 hash routes, `/`, `/es/`, `/pt-br/` and `/labs/` (text nodes, the ARIA, `title`,
`placeholder`, `alt`, `value` and `content` attributes, form values, `document.title`, `<html lang dir>` and `localStorage`, and
the help viewer on the 36 loads that offer one): 72 loads, about 159,000 strings, every one identical once React's `useId` values and
loading placeholders are set aside, and no bidi control; every file of `dist/` outside `assets/` byte-identical but `index.html`, which
differs only in asset hashes. Under `qps-rtl`, with and without View original, Save investigation on seven routes writes the same file
name and SHA-256 as English, and no bidi control reaches the file, `document.title` or `localStorage`.
Review verification: `tsc -b`; Vitest `src/i18n`, `src/components`, `src/help`, `src/theme` and `src/seo` 961 in 43 files; the pin
refresh leaves the pins unchanged; `npm run build` with every check; Playwright `shell`, `language-selector`, `exports-identity`,
`render-budget` and `scripts-pseudo.smoke` 61; `npm run test:scripts` 85 (83, and the two popup-menu placements that timed out while
another front's suite ran passed alone).

## French: the folder, lints and shell (FR0)

The first front of French (`fr`), plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-fr.md` §1–§6 "FR0" in the research root, following SC8's
"Adding a language" and PT0. User decisions of 2026-11-11 (AGENTS.md): one neutral international French, tag `fr`; «vous»; U+202F
between thousands, before ; : ! ? and inside « », the decimal point kept, files unchanged. French stays hidden (decision 5 of
2026-11-09): only a `?lang=fr` preview reaches it until FR-REL. Its translations are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's.

**The registry line.** `{"tag":"fr","dir":"fr","catalog":"fr.json","budgetFactor":1,"htmlLang":"fr","pagePath":"fr","autonym":"Français",
"englishName":"French","match":["fr"],"released":false,"thousands":"\u202f","notices":{…}}`, no `help` yet. `thousands` is set from
the first front (PT-REL had to edit 28 literals late). Once released, every `fr` and `fr-*` browser in any case (fr-FR, fr-CA, fr-BE,
FR-ch, fr-SN…) opens it; `frr`, `fur` and `br` do not; `?lang=FR`, `?lang=fr-FR` and `?lang=fr-CA` are no tag. The notices read
«Impossible de charger le français ; le site s’affiche en anglais. Rechargez la page pour réessayer.» and «Impossible de charger une
partie du texte en français ; cette partie s’affiche en anglais. Rechargez la page pour réessayer.», with U+202F before «;» written
as the character. `tests/fixtures/i18n/registry.ts` takes this line instead of its stand-in.

**The folder** `src/i18n/fr/`, mirroring PT0's: `index.ts` (`FRENCH`, the manifest the planner parses), `fr.json`, `originals.json`,
`maps/`, `glossary.json`, `identical.json`, `lint-allowed.json` and `fr.test.ts`.
- **Translated:** PT0's shell slice, the 130 catalog keys (1,454 English words: `app.*`, `project.*`, `session.*`, `error.*`,
  `instructorMode.*`, every `*.nav`, the home and first steps, `demonstrationMode.banner`, `spectrum.tools`, `classLink.cancel`,
  `app.documentTitle`); the complete maps `appShell` (with HEADER's three `pageBar` strings), `helpMenu`, `languageChoice` (legend
  «Langue / Language»), `projectLinks` and `tour` (the save step names the top of the page and the menu beside it); the partial maps
  `objectXLab` (`nav`, `guideNav`), `polish` (`clusterGuide.nav`, `results.help`) and `variableStars` (`nav`); the 10 VIREO captions
  the shell shows (Help «Aide», Topics... «Rubriques...», Websites «Sites web», 2MASS Catalog «Catalogue 2MASS», About This
  Exercise... «À propos de cet exercice...», Results Editors «Éditeurs de résultats», Observational Results «Résultats
  d’observation», Pulsar Results «Résultats de pulsars»; Project CLEA and NVO kept). FR1 translates all 744 originals.
- **Style** (plan §3): «vous»; buttons and menu items in the infinitive («Enregistrer l’investigation», «Annuler»), instructions as
  vous-imperatives («Cliquez», «Rechargez la page»); sentence case; accented capitals; a lab is «TP» («Guide du TP Hubble»); the tour
  is «visite guidée»; instructor mode «mode enseignant», Demonstration Mode «Mode démonstration» with VIREO's English in brackets at
  the first mention in prose; «le CLEA», «du Project CLEA». The phone menu button is «Menu» (as pt), the analyser's sidebar «Outils».
- **How it was translated:** three Sonnet 5.5 batches (`model: "sonnet"`, from the English, the Spanish and Portuguese shown only as
  meaning context, one brief with the plan's §3–§5): the 130 keys (`scripts/i18n-batch.mjs merge --lang fr`), the eight maps with the
  10 originals, and the glossary seed. The batches wrote ordinary spaces before ; : ! ? and inside « », and ’ for the apostrophe; the
  fixer (below) wrote U+202F. The Opus implementer read every row and changed 15 strings: the radio receiver's Record as «une
  acquisition du récepteur (Record)» (Record is «Consigner» only for a measurement; «enregistrement» is Save), the photometer's
  «Consigner/Revoir (Record/Review)» glossed, «ne peut en contenir» twice, «du Project CLEA», the document title without «Les»,
  `instructorMode.confirm.what` without nested brackets and with «Options de simulation optique (Optical Simulation Options)», «le
  contexte de l’aperçu de l’observatoire» twice, «parties» for the "scopes" an earlier file keeps (twice), «le relevé LSS», and «Guide
  de l’Objet X»; and seven glossary names (below).
- **Glossary** (`glossary.json`, `fr` field, `glossaryFrom(…,'fr')`): the Portuguese glossary's 148 terms and 180 names re-targeted to
  French from the English, plus 10 names from the plan's §5.2 (Instructor mode, Lab Table, and exact Clear, Find, Record, Show, Save,
  Save As, Save List..., Find Pulsar..., Print). The implementer set Find Text String «Rechercher du texte» and Find Pulsar Data
  «Localiser les données du pulsar» (plan §5.2), Show Object ID «Afficher l’identifiant de l’objet», Record/Review
  «Consigner/Revoir», Record Z «Consigner z», and U+202F in «Le télescope a été déplacé !» and «Lecture du ciel !». Five `forbidden`
  rows: «ligne» for a spectral line, cluster, redshift, «décalage (au|en|dans le) rouge», «supporté». FR1 reviews the VIREO menu names
  before using them; loose stems that FR1–FR6 should tighten as their areas arrive: count, load, slew, bin, seeing, merge, turn-off
  («point de coude|coude|point de sortie|point de virage|turn-off», plan §5.1 leaves it to FR4), exposure («pose|exposition»).

**The French typography fixer** (`src/i18n/lint/frenchSpacing.ts`, pure, idempotent; `frenchSpacing.test.ts`, 45 tests with the review's two). It writes
U+202F before ; : ! ? and inside « », replacing an ordinary space or U+00A0; ’ between two letters (or a letter and a value); straight
"…" pairs and top-level “…” as « … » (inner “ ” kept inside « »); U+202F between thousands. It leaves alone (plan §4.2): VIREO English
glosses in brackets (an ASCII span that opens with a capital or a digit and holds no French elision: «(RA:)», «(Observer's
Notebook)», «(Save As...)»; «(par exemple : 1.5)» and «(Voir l’aide)» are French and are fixed), `{placeholders}` and the lint's
«A1» samples, URLs and `?lang=`, times, ratios and paths («12:30:45», «1:2», «C:\»: a mark not followed by a space, the end or a
closer), a mark after another (`?!`) or after ( or [, and the key in «la touche ?». A VIREO `fragment|` original keeps its straight
quotes (`quotes:false`). `fixSourceLiterals` rewrites only the string literals of a map or help source (single- and double-quoted,
templates with each `${…}` as one value mark and the code inside rewritten the same way), never comments, regular expressions, object
keys or import paths, and writes the characters themselves (PT-REL's lesson: `check-i18n-dist.mjs` reads map literals as written).
- **Fix mode:** `env -u CI OPENVIREO_FIX_TYPOGRAPHY=1 npx vitest run src/i18n/fr/fr.test.ts` rewrites `fr.json`, `originals.json`,
  `maps/*.ts` and `help/*.ts`; run it once more without the variable to check (the test's imports were read before the rewrite).
  Each FR front: batches → fix mode → strict lint.

**The `fr` lint profile** (`src/i18n/lint/profiles.ts`, beside pt-BR). Latin script, letter boundaries, French case folding, plural
categories one/many/other with `many` optional (CLDR: 0 and 1.5 are `one`, exact millions `many`; no English override as pt has).
- **Spacing, apostrophes and quotes are the fixer's own view:** a new additive hook in `rules.ts`, `LocaleProfile.typographyCheck`,
  counts under `typography` every change the fixer would still make, so the lint fails exactly what the fixer leaves and they cannot
  disagree. Thousands: PT-REL's two pt-BR patterns became the shared `thousandsRules(sep)`, used by pt-BR and fr.
- **Register «vous»** (`REGISTER.fr`, «tu» expressible): tu, te, toi, ton, ta, tes, tien(ne)(s) (case-sensitive word lists) and the
  elided «t’»; and pair-aware tu-imperatives (Clique, Choisis, Ouvre, Enregistre… at a sentence start, counted only when the English
  sentence starts with a bare imperative, PT0's `register.paired`), so «Mesure la largeur…» for "Measures the width…" passes.
- **Typography errors:** elision («le/la/de/que/ne/se/ce/je/me/jusque/lorsque/puisque» before a word of two or more letters that starts
  with a vowel, and «si il(s)»; h and y excluded; a pronoun after a hyphen, «Arrêtez-le avec», is no article), sentence-initial «A »
  for «À», unaccented capital É in «Etat, Etoile, Echelle, Ecran, Etape, Ecart, Element…», missing œ (coeur, oeil, oeuvre, manoeuvre,
  soeur, noeud, voeu, oeuf), the point médian between letters, Spanish and Portuguese words and characters left from the context
  (usted, también, você, não, archivo, arquivo, cúmulo, aglomerado, estrella, estrela, pantalla, tela, medición, medição; ñ ¿ ¡ ã õ),
  false friends and anglicisms (supporté…, librairie, digital…, cluster, redshift), English function words (pt's list), title case
  (three capitalised words in a row, French connectors between, outside brackets). Words outside brackets, “ ” and « » (glosses).
- **Marks:** « » must balance; straight `"` and ‘ are forbidden marks (’ is the apostrophe). **Claims:** «traduction officielle»,
  «traduction du CLEA», «texte du CLEA»; denials («ce n’est pas une traduction officielle», «traduction non officielle», «ce n’est pas
  un texte du CLEA») pass.
- **Fixtures** (`fixtures.ts`, `fr`): 49 (48 and the review's gloss row), a good and a bad string per rule, the register both ways, every typography row, the §4.2
  exceptions as a passing case and the French-in-brackets case as a failing one.
- **Calibration** on the 130 shell keys (grapheme ratio, English over 12 graphemes, 113 strings): minimum 0.89, 5th percentile 1.00,
  median 1.22, 95th percentile 1.53, maximum 2.00 (`classLink.cancel` «Annuler l’enregistrement ou la restauration en attente»);
  against Spanish median 1.04, against Portuguese 1.07. The band stays 0.7–1.7 (reported, not failed).
- `src/i18n/fr/fr.test.ts`: the registry line (`released:false`, `thousands`, the notices fixed), a valid partial catalog covering the
  130 shell keys, map shapes (five complete, three partial), originals Spanish also translates, the fixer a no-op over the catalog,
  originals and every map and help source and no `\u202f`/`\u2019`/`\u00a0` escape there, `formatNumber` with U+202F (24 000,
  2 400 000, 1 234.5, −31 147, no grouping with `useGrouping:false`, English «24,000»), the CLDR plural rows, and `lintPairs` with the
  profile, glossary, `identical.json` (6 rows) and `lint-allowed.json` (empty): no errors.

**The per-area test gate** (plan §1.2 #1). `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `Area` (`shell`, `observatory`, `e01`–`e05`, `radio`, `e06`,
`e08`, `help`), each language's `done:ReadonlySet<Area>` (every area for a released language, which the helper requires), and
`translatedFor(area)`. Every parameterised spec loops `translatedFor('<its area>')` instead of `TRANSLATED` (`shell`, `observatory`,
`e01`–`e06`, `e08`, `radio`; `help` keeps its `entry.help` filter on top), so registering French runs only the shell spec in French.
`FRENCH_TEXT` (`?lang=fr`, letters `/[àâæçéèêëîïôœùûüÿ«»\u202f]/i`, `done:{shell}`); each FR front adds its area and its map rows
(`E01_E02_MAPS`, `E04_E05_MAPS`, `RADIO_MAPS`, `E06_MAPS`, `E08_MAPS`, `HELP_TEXT`). `narrow` and `browsers` keep their
released-only filter. `npx playwright test --list` over `tests/i18n`: every Spanish and Portuguese title unchanged.

**Tests.**
- `shell.spec.ts` runs French (13 tests) in an en-US browser with `?lang=fr`; its first title reads "a ?lang=fr preview fetches…" for
  a language before its release.
- `areas.spec.ts`: PT0's end-to-end preview case comes back for French, "a partial language (French) fetches only its core, and no
  chunk for an empty area" (core on the home page; on each of the 11 area routes exactly the non-empty modules of French's plan —
  only `photometer`, which holds `project.photometryCleared` and `project.photometryRestored` — with English route text, no notice,
  no route error, no read before its area). FR-REL turns it into a released-language test, as PT-REL did.
- `language-selector.spec.ts`: fr, fr-FR, fr-CA, FR-ch and fr-BE browsers and a stored fr get English with three options; `?lang=fr`
  gets French, its title and four options with their `lang`; a change there stores nothing and travels as `?lang=`; and the keyboard
  test across four options (arrows, Home, End, Enter, Escape, Tab).
- `exports-identity.spec.ts`, additive: `Locale` gains `fr`, and the toolbar Save investigation family runs in English, qps, Spanish,
  Brazilian Portuguese and French (same name and SHA-256; no « », ’, U+202F or French letter in the file).
- `tests/fixtures/language.ts`: `FRENCH_LOCALE` (fr-FR), used by `contextLocale` once French is released.
- Vitest: `locale.test.ts` (French before its release: fr, fr-FR, fr-CA, FR-ch, fr-BE browsers and a stored fr → English, `?lang=fr`
  → fr, `FR`/`fr-FR`/`fr-CA`/`fr-fr` no tag; after a simulated release fr-* → fr, frr/fur/br → English), `languages.test.ts`
  (`[es-419 ✓, pt-BR ✓, fr ✗]`, French's notices, fr-ES now asks for French), `direction.test.ts`, `pageBar.test.ts` («Plus» starts
  «Plus d’actions sur l’investigation»), `rules.test.ts` (eight profiles, `fr:'vous'`), `src/seo/languages.test.ts` (a language
  before its release needs over 100 markers, a released one over 1,000; French has about 160).

**Sizes** (`npm run build`, `check-i18n-dist.mjs`, against main 88576b4 built the same way, chunk hashes normalised). fr: 2 chunks,
8.6 KB gzip — core 8.3 KB gzip (25.0 KB raw), photometer 0.3 KB; seven areas "empty: no text yet, no chunk". Spanish and Portuguese:
every language and help chunk byte-identical. English: `index.html` and every other file identical but for chunk hashes, except the
shared chunk with the registry (`jsx-runtime`, +428 B raw, +126 B gzip: the fr line) and the entry (`index`, +143 B raw, +28 B gzip:
its loader). Deploy limits: 2,168 files.

**Verification** (2026-11-10, worktree `claude/front-fr0` from main 88576b4). `npx tsc -b`; Vitest over `src/i18n` (with `fr.test.ts`,
`frenchSpacing.test.ts` and the lint fixtures), `src/help`, `src/seo` and `src/components`: 1,079 tests in 42 files; the pins
refreshed with no change; `npm run build` with every check. Playwright (dev server, Chromium, DejaVu fontconfig, two workers):
`shell` and `language-selector` 45, `areas` and `observatory` 41, `exports-identity` 21; `npm run test:scripts` 81; `npm run
test:pages` on `dist/` 74. On `dist/` served from loopback: fr-FR and fr-CA browsers without `?lang=` opened English with three
options; `?lang=fr` (en-US and fr-FR browsers) opened French, `<html lang="fr">`, the French title and four options. No failures.

**Independent review** (2026-11-10, Opus, high effort; head 6776847).
- *Fixer, medium:* `fixSourceLiterals` decoded a quoted literal's escapes as `\\(.)` → that letter, so fix mode would have written a
  batch's `'Prêt\u00a0?'` as «Prêtu00a0 ?», `\t` as «t» and `\x41` as «x41» without any check failing (the no-escape test then
  passes). It now decodes every JavaScript escape (`\n \r \t \b \f \v \0`, `\uXXXX`, `\u{…}`, `\xXX`, line continuations) and writes
  the characters, escaping only the backslash, its own quote and control characters; in a template, a `\u`, `\x`, `\'` or `\"`
  escape is seen as its character, while `` \` ``, `\\`, `\$` and control escapes stay. Two tests.
- *Lint, low:* an ASCII bracket opening with a capital counted as a VIREO English gloss whatever it held, so French there («(Par
  exemple: 12)») escaped both the fixer and the lint; `helpMenu.websitesNote` had one («(NASA Astronomical Virtual Observatories, les
  observatoires virtuels astronomiques de la NASA)»). `typographyCheck` now receives the English source (`rules.ts`), and the `fr`
  check fails a kept gloss whose text the English does not hold (`frenchGlosses`; one fixture row, one test).
- *Fix mode, low:* `OPENVIREO_FIX_TYPOGRAPHY=1` is refused when `CI` is set, as `OPENVIREO_REFRESH_PINS` is: in CI it would have
  rewritten the sources and then checked its own output.
- *Hidden, test:* the FR0 selector test also asserts that fr, fr-FR, fr-CA, FR-ch, fr-BE browsers and a stored fr fetch no French
  module (`i18n-fr-*` or `src/i18n/fr/`).
- *French, 9 corrections:* `instructorMode.confirm.session` «Il n’est enregistré ni dans les investigations, ni dans les fichiers,
  ni dans ce navigateur»; the Radio clock and timers «suspendus» (not «retenus») during Save, twice (`project.saved`,
  `project.toolbarHelp`); `project.photometryRestored` «Activez-la et acquérez une nouvelle mesure du ciel» (not «un nouveau ciel»);
  `helpMenu.websitesNote` the NAVO gloss alone in brackets with the French as an apposition; `helpMenu.exitNote` «après Exécuter
  l’exercice (Run Exercise)» without nested brackets; glossary No More Spectra «Aucun autre spectre» («Plus de spectres» also reads
  "more spectra"), Access Pulsar List «Accéder à la liste de pulsars» (a menu item in the infinitive), turn-off also «point de
  coude» (plan §5.1). Read and kept: every other catalog key, map string, original and glossary row (vous throughout, U+202F and ’
  as characters, sentence case, accented capitals, IAU OAE terms, no claim). Uncertain, for FR4: «diagramme en coin» for the wedge
  plot (French outreach also writes «diagramme en cône»); «2 MiB» kept as es and pt keep it («Mio» is the French IEC symbol).
- *CI, 4c92a81 (run 42962021928):* `help.spec.ts` "the Spectral Line Table is Spanish…" failed twice on shard 4/4 (the Spanish help
  shown after switching to the original; on the retry, no help at all). Not caused by FR0 (its only edit there is the
  `translatedFor('help')` filter, the same es/pt set): a latent race on main. `SpectralLineTable.tsx`'s view switch fetches before it
  lands and closes the help window, and the test double-clicked a line before that, so the line read in the translation finished
  after the switch (or the switch closed what it opened). The component now drops a line read begun before a switch lands
  (`viewGeneration`); the test waits for the original label and the closed help, as the other help tests do; a new test slows both
  reads so the translation's arrives last (it fails without the fix). `help.spec.ts` ×3: 66 passed.
- *No regression:* the pt-BR profile's patterns are identical before and after `thousandsRules` (only the hint now shows «24 000»
  with U+202F), and every pt-BR and es-419 catalog/originals finding and fixture verdict is byte-identical to main 88576b4;
  `playwright test --list`: 1,614 → 1,628, all 14 new tests French, every Spanish and Portuguese title unchanged (one toolbar title
  renamed to add French).

**Left for later fronts.** FR1: the observatory and all 744 originals, `vireoSites` strict for fr, the VIREO menu names of the
glossary (and its loose stems). FR2–FR6: their areas in `FRENCH_TEXT.done` with their map rows, and their export families in fr.
FR-HELP: help files, `help.related` «RUBRIQUES CONNEXES», `LATIN1_HELP.fr` and the ’/œ search fold. FR-REL+SEO: `released`, the
partial-language Playwright case back to the qaa-XA fixture, the released-language gate rows of `languages.test.ts` moved to `id`,
the quadrilingual 404 and the search pages.

## Script groundwork: exports and deep walks under the script pseudo-locales (SC10, SC12)

Fronts SC10 and SC12 (plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-scripts.md` §§ 4 item 5, 10 and the SC10 and SC12 rows of § 12),
together. Tests only (the review then changed one stylesheet rule for the script languages, below). English, Spanish and
Portuguese screens and every file the site writes are unchanged.

- **One name matcher for every pseudo-locale** (`tests/fixtures/pseudoNames.ts`). `pseudoName(template, params)` matches an English
  catalog, map or VIREO text in English, in `qps` (the specs' old accent regex, which also matches a map function's result, only
  marked) and in `qps-cjk`, `qps-deva`, `qps-cyrl` and `qps-rtl`, computed with the app's own pure transforms
  (`PSEUDO_SCRIPT`): the template is transformed and its `{params}` filled afterwards, as the app does, with the ⟦ ⟧ · marks,
  spacing and bidi controls allowed between characters (composed names, and the spaces `qps-cjk` drops beside full-width text).
  `pseudoStart` is the prefix form. `isPseudo`, `PSEUDO_HTML_LANG`, `htmlLangOf` and `PSEUDO_LETTERS` (each variant's script) go
  with it. No hard-coded pseudo text anywhere.
- **The walks moved into modules** (`tests/i18n/walks/`). The walk of each language-parameterised spec, with the helpers it needs
  (`N`, `texts`, `same`, its `STEPS`, its `LAYOUT` options, and the observatory's Multi-Spec walk), moved unchanged from
  `e01`–`e06`, `e08`, `observatory` and `radio` `.spec.ts` into `tests/i18n/walks/<name>.ts`; each spec imports what its tests
  use. A spec cannot import another spec, so this is what lets the scripts config run the same walks. The only edits: `Lang` is
  `'en' | Pseudo | LanguageText`, the qps branch is `isPseudo(lang)` with `pseudoName`, the expected `<html lang>` is `htmlLangOf`,
  two hard-coded qps regexes (Telescope, Ask VizieR live) gained the script forms, and file URLs gained one `../`. The help spec
  has no step walk; `tests/i18n/walks/help.ts` is one over its `WINDOWS` list (now shared with the spec): the nine instrument
  help windows' topic lists and first topics, the radio Help topics, the Exit Observatory reminder, the Spectral Line Table and
  its help, and OpenVireo's help with a topic.

### SC10: files the site writes, under the script pseudo-locales

- `tests/i18n/exports-identity.spec.ts`: `identical()` runs every family that runs in qps, that is **all 21 families**, also in
  `qps-cjk`, `qps-deva`, `qps-cyrl` and `qps-rtl` (dev server), and the shell toolbar test loops over them too. Each must give
  the English file name and SHA-256 (after the family's documented masks), no ⟦ in the bytes, `<html lang>` of the variant, and no
  letter of the variant's script in a file that is UTF-8 text (a hot list's binary doubles can decode to a CJK character by
  chance, so binary files are compared whole and searched for ⟦ only). `L` is now `pseudoName`, so the flows needed only four
  regex edits (Record measurement, Telescope, Filter, Ask VizieR live).
- **Result: every family is byte-identical in all four variants.** No writer reads the screen language.
- **One flow changed, for qps-rtl:** the spectrum family pressed ArrowRight on the z slider. Under `qps-rtl` the native range
  input runs right to left, so ArrowRight lowered z and the CSV differed (`-0.015625`): a different input, not a writer that
  follows the language. The flow presses ArrowUp, which raises one step in either direction; English is unchanged. **For RT5:**
  every native `type=range` slider of the site (the z slider, LSS shift, the cluster HR modulus, E(B−V), age and offset…)
  reverses under `dir=rtl`; they are data controls and should be left-to-right islands.
- **Runtime:** the spec took 3.2 min with en, qps, es-419 and pt-BR, and 6.9 min with the four variants (2 workers, this machine).
  All four variants run, not a subset. Since the review each (family × language) is its own test, and the file runs in two CI
  parts of its own (review item 6).

### SC12: the deep walks under the script pseudo-locales

- `tests/scripts/walks.scripts.ts` (scripts config, SC1 test fonts, `npm run test:scripts`). Eleven walks (E01, E02, E03, E04, E05,
  E06, E08, the observatory, its Multi-Spec windows, the radio telescope, and the help windows) under `qps-cjk`, `qps-deva` and
  `qps-cyrl` at 320 and 1440 px and `qps-rtl` at 320 px: 77 tests. After each step:
  - the **pseudo marker**: every visible text, option, accessible name, title and placeholder that still has two Latin letters is
    marked ⟦…⟧, outside the spec's own identifiers (its `same`, or E06's `data`) and native English mirrors: the spec's qps check,
    with its `texts`. A print preview's date line is exempt in every variant (`screenDateTime` writes the page language's format,
    and CLDR Hindi writes `am`/`pm` in Latin; the qps checks exempt the same line in English's format). The help walk reads only
    the help windows and leaves out the help file's own lines (VIREO's English under every pseudo-locale);
  - `layoutProblems()` with the spec's own `LAYOUT` options and `verticalInkProblems()` (`tests/fixtures/layoutProblems.ts`);
  - no page error during the walk.
- **qps-rtl, before mirroring (RT5):** its walks must complete, with the marker, the ink check and no page error; of
  `layoutProblems` only `… clipped` (a control whose own overflow cuts its text, whichever side) is asserted. Page overflow,
  leaving the viewport, crossing a card's edge and plot text depend on which side things are placed and wait for RT5 and the
  RT7 walk. At 320 px only: the one layout check asserted is width-sensitive, and the others are not. Every `qps-rtl` walk passes.
- **`KNOWN`** works as in SC3: an entry names the walk, variants, widths, step, problem, file and reason; an unlisted problem fails,
  and a required entry that stops appearing fails. A walk that cannot go on is recorded as `walk stopped: …` at the last step it
  completed, so it can be listed too. `fontDependent` entries (within 2 px, font files differ between Arch and CI) are tolerated
  but not required. The entries, all for the next script front (none fixed here: each needs a component or shared stylesheet
  change that alters the English and Spanish layout, or a file the parallel front RT4 edits; the E03 one was fixed in review):
  - **E02, `qps-cyrl`, 320 px, wedge row selected — page overflow.** `src/components/Wedge.css` `.wedge-details` sizes its label
    column `max-content`, so the longest label never wraps and leaves the value column no room.
  - **E06, `qps-cyrl`, 320 px, recording loaded — page overflow, a header leaves the viewport.** The receiver properties table
    (`.plr-props-table`, `PulsarAnalysis.css`/`.tsx`) is not in a sideways scroller and an unbreakable header word widens it.
  - **Radio, `qps-cyrl`, 320 px — a legend crosses its fieldset** in Radio Simulation Options (`RadioAccessOptions.tsx`,
    `RadioControlPanel.css`), and **the Record Data properties box scrolls sideways** (`RadioReceiverWindows.css`): one long word.
  - **E03, `qps-deva`, 320 px — the comparison cursor kept no reading** (`walk stopped`; **fixed in review**, entry removed).
    Cause, measured in review: the cursor read-out above the plot (`.comparison-cursor-live`) is a fixed 86 px in English, but
    `src/theme/scripts.css` § 6 let it grow for ja, zh, hi and ru (`height:auto; min-height:86px`). At Hindi's 1.7 line height
    its three lines need 87.2 px, so when the press showed a reading the box grew by 1.17 px, the plot moved, scroll anchoring
    scrolled 1 px and left a 0.17 px shift, and the held press was cancelled (the surface cancels on any document scroll, and
    its release also refuses a changed screen transform). Fix: the rule's `min-height` is now `max(86px, calc(3lh + 26px))`,
    three lines at the script's own line height plus padding and border, so a reading no longer resizes the box. No released
    language was affected (English, Spanish, Portuguese and French keep the fixed 86 px). Still open, for the Hindi front: a
    reading that needs a fourth line in a real translation would grow the box again; the walk at 320 px will show it.
  - **Multi-Spec (font-dependent, tolerated):** VIREO's `1.0`/`0.0` axis labels pass through `vireo()`, so every pseudo-locale
    wraps them in ⟦ ⟧ and the end-anchored label starts 1–4 px left of the SVG (an artifact: a real translation keeps `1.0`); the
    rotated y-axis title crosses the left edge by about 1 px under `qps-cjk` at 1440 px, as the Pleiades and cluster titles did
    before SC11; and the selection number `3` reaches 2 px above the Multi-Spec field under `qps-cjk` (`src/core/overlayLabels.ts`).
    `keepInsideSvg` (`src/plotTextFit.ts`) is the fix for the first two in `src/multispec/MultiSpecDisplay.tsx`, left because RT4
    edits SVG text.
- **Real findings that are not layout:** none of the walks shows a text that misses the catalog, a map or `vireo()` under the
  script variants beyond what the qps walks already exempt. The help viewer leaves VIREO's English help rows untagged
  (`textLang` in `src/clusters/CleaHelp.tsx` is `en` only in the original view), so a page in a language without translated
  help files shows English rows under its own `lang`; no released language is in that state (each translates the help files).
- **Runtime** (2 workers, this machine): `npm run test:scripts` went from 81 tests in 3.2 min to 158 in 12.4 min, so the 77 walks
  add about 9 min (88 walks with `qps-rtl` at both widths took 12.9 min on their own). The CI job `scripts` takes about 5 min
  today (`timeout-minutes: 30`); at the same ratio it would take about 19 min. Split in two in review (below).
- **Verification.** `tsc -b`; Vitest `src/i18n` 601 in 23 files; `npm run build` with every check. Playwright, Chromium: the moved
  specs (`tests/i18n` `e01`–`e06`, `e08`, `observatory`, `radio`, `help`, `shell`; dev server, DejaVu fontconfig) 167 of 167;
  `exports-identity.spec.ts` (en, qps, es-419, pt-BR and the four variants) 21 of 21 (its first run found the two issues above); `npm run test:scripts` (SC1 test
  fonts) 158 of 158, twice (the first while the exports spec ran beside it).

### SC10/SC12 independent review (claude-opus-5-5, high effort)

Raw findings, then resolutions.

1. *Moved walks.* `playwright test --list` of the moved specs, `shell` and `exports-identity` gives the same 188 titles as main
   88576b4 (one renamed: the shell toolbar test names the script pseudo-locales). Line by line, the walk modules plus the specs equal
   main's specs except the edits listed above; the qps matcher is the old one (same accents, marks and anchors, now also allowing
   bidi controls), and the observatory's Multi-Spec walk is not run in qps by any spec. **No change needed.**
2. *SC10.* Every family runs in the four variants (all twenty `identical()` calls include `qps`; the toolbar test loops them) and
   compares name and masked SHA-256 with English. ArrowUp raises a horizontal range input exactly as ArrowRight does in a
   left-to-right page, so the English, Spanish and Portuguese runs are unchanged. The UTF-8 filter cannot hide a leak: the letter
   check runs on the same masked bytes whose SHA-256 must equal English's, so it is already implied by the hash. **No change.**
   **Doc error, fixed above:** the 21 tests do not spread over the e2e shards; all land in shard 4/4. Shard 4 took 40.9 min of
   its 60 in the branch's CI run (37.2 and 45.2 min in the two main runs before it): within the limit, but the shard with the
   least room.
3. *KNOWN.* Each entry names walk, variants, widths, step (exact, or a pattern for the font-dependent Multi-Spec ones), problem,
   file and an owner (the Russian or next script front); the required ones fail when they disappear. **No change.**
4. *E03, `qps-deva`, 320 px (high: a student's press on the comparison spectrum never kept a reading).* Confirmed, but the cause
   is not the scroll listener alone (see the corrected KNOWN item above): `src/theme/scripts.css` § 6 let the cursor read-out
   grow, a reading added 1.17 px, and both the scroll listener and the release's screen-transform check then cancel. Making the
   listener ignore a scroll that leaves the surface in place, as `ComparisonFeatureSurface` and `FeatureCursor` do, was tried
   first and is not enough (the transform moved by 0.17 px). **Fixed** at the cause (`min-height:max(86px, calc(3lh + 26px))`,
   Vitest `scripts.test.ts` updated); the KNOWN entry is removed, so the full E03 walk under `qps-deva` at 320 px is now a
   required check, and it passes. Seen in the browser: the read-out stays 87.2 px with its three lines whole. No released
   language was affected.
5. *CI time.* **Done:** the `scripts` job is two parts (`strategy.matrix.part: [1, 2]`, each with its own fonts install, cache
   step and 30-minute limit; artifacts `scripts-results-1`/`-2`; `deploy` still needs `scripts`, which waits for both).
   Playwright's `--shard=1/2` would put all 77 deep walks in the second part (it splits by test count in file order, and
   `walks.scripts.ts` sorts last), so the parts are complementary `--grep`/`--grep-invert` selections: part 2 is the walks under
   `qps-deva`, `qps-cyrl` and `qps-rtl` (55 tests), part 1 everything else (103). `--list` confirms they are disjoint and cover
   all 158. Locally (2 workers) the deva and cyrl walks took 5.8 min and the rest 8.6 min before the rtl walks moved over (about
   1.4 min). The unsharded job took 21.1 min in CI.
6. *Exports identity in CI (high: the e2e job failed).* With French and the four script variants each family ran eight flows
   inside one test, and the families without their own timeout (spectrum analysis, options file, pulsar results, star .SSP) passed
   30 s; all 21 tests sat in e2e shard 4/4, which took 51 min. **Fixed structurally:** `family(title, locales, body)` makes each
   (family × variant) its own test, `<family> [<variant>]`: it runs the variant's flow and compares it with English's files byte
   for byte as before, after the same masks. English runs once per worker and family (`ENGLISH`, module state: a fresh context
   and fixed clock, so deterministic) and is shared by that family's tests in the worker; a test runs two flows at most. The
   toolbar family is split the same way. 141 tests (was 21): 16 families × 7 variants, the CCD camera × 6, three qps-only
   families × 5, the toolbar × 8 (French there only). Sharding still cuts the test list in file order, so the file would sit in
   one shard; the `e2e` job is now six matrix parts: four general shards with the file filtered out
   (`--grep-invert="exports-identity\.spec" --shard=k/4`: 409, 416, 396 and 404 tests) and the file in two
   (`tests/i18n/exports-identity.spec.ts --shard=k/2`: 71 and 70). `--list` confirms the six parts cover all 1766 tests once.
   Locally (2 workers) the file took 7.9 min whole, 2.8 and 5.0 min as its two parts; the per-part CI times are in the
   coordinator's report.
7. *Merge of main (RT4, HOME, FR0).* The parameterised specs keep the walk modules with FR0's per-area gate (`translatedFor`, the
   same count per spec as main); HOME's welcome step moved into `tests/i18n/walks/observatory.ts`, so the deep walks now check the
   welcome under every script pseudo-locale too; `help.spec.ts` keeps FR0's Spectral Line Table test and imports the shared
   `WINDOWS` (the help walk does not switch views, so the race does not apply to it); exports identity keeps French and the
   script variants. Under RT4's isolates every `qps-rtl` walk still passes, and so do the scripts parts (55 and 107 with RT4's
   bidi tests).

## RTL groundwork: logical CSS and direction-aware placement (RT5, RT6)

Fronts RT5 and RT6 (plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-rtl.md` § 1, § 2, § 6.1 and the RT5/RT6 rows of § 9). Groundwork only:
no right-to-left language is registered, so only the dev-only `qps-rtl` (`<html lang="ar" dir="rtl">`) reaches any of it. **Nothing
moves for a left-to-right visitor** (the proof is below). The rule stays: **mirror the page, never the data.**

- **RT5, logical CSS.** The 146 physical inline-axis declarations of 28 English sheets (the plan counted 141; the scan also counts
  the page-bar chevron's `border-width` and four declarations split across shorthands) are now: 139 logical — `margin-`/`padding-`
  `inline-start/end`, `border-inline-start/end` (and `-width`), `inset-inline-start/end`, `text-align:start/end` (numeric columns
  `end`: they align left in a right-to-left table, as right-to-left spreadsheets do), and the asymmetric shorthands as
  `*-block` + `*-inline` pairs (`padding:8px 12px 8px 16px` → `padding-block:8px;padding-inline:16px 12px`, `inset:0 auto 0 0` →
  `inset-block:0;inset-inline:0 auto`), each in its original place so the cascade order is unchanged — and 7 kept physical, each
  with a `/* physical: … */` note in its rule: the photographic strip's and the combined trace's 20 px margins (inside plots),
  `.native-slit`'s `left` (over the star frame), `.em-caption`'s symmetric `left`/`right` (over the sky), and the two chevrons whose
  borders draw their shape (the page bar's backup disclosure, which points down and is not flipped, and the research notes, whose
  closed rotation `rtl.css` mirrors). So the fixed sidebar (`inset-inline`, `border-inline-end`, the body's `padding-inline-start`),
  the page bar (place at the right, tools at the left; its menus `inset-inline-end:0`), callout bars, list indents, table
  alignment, dialog close buttons and the cluster member window mirror by themselves.
- **`src/theme/rtl.css`** (imported once by `src/main.tsx`, after `scripts.css`; every selector starts at `:root[dir=rtl]` or names
  `:lang(ar)`, `:lang(fa)` or `:lang(he)`):
  - *Left-to-right islands* (`direction:ltr;unicode-bidi:isolate`): `svg` (plots, the sky map, icons), `canvas` (also `ctx.direction`,
    whose default is `inherit`, so the radio receiver's canvas labels need no `.tsx` change), `pre`, `[lang=en]` (native mirrors and
    VIREO originals), range sliders and their end labels (`.range-labels`), the photographic strip's scroller (`.photo-scroll`, whose
    `scrollLeft` keeps its sign), the image viewports and sky overlays (`.fits-viewport`, `.fits-header`, `.native-star-frame`,
    `.em-layer`, `.ne-sky`, `.ms-field`), the antenna pad (`.antenna-manual-pad`: East stays left of West) and the spectrum's ◀ ▶
    buttons (`.engine-buttons`), and VIREO's fixed-column native rows that carry no `lang="en"` (the optical and radio
    hot-list rows `.ob-row-native` and `.antenna-hotlist-native`, the hot list's print lines `.ob-print-lines`, the Multi-Spec
    list `.ms-items` and its information lines `.ms-lines`: added by the review, after a radio hot-list row such as
    `0320+39   3h 23m 26.61s …` showed its ID at the end under `qps-rtl`); number, decimal and numeric inputs are left to right
    and aligned at the right. Every range slider is
    an island (the wavelength range, z, the LSS shift, the cluster HR sliders and the zoom): a native slider reverses under
    `dir=rtl`, and each of these values has a physical direction.
  - *Flips*: the page bar's separator (`scaleX(-1)`), a collapsed menu section's chevron (`rotate(90deg)` for English's
    `-90deg`) and a closed research-notes chevron (`rotate(135deg)` for `-45deg`). Not flipped: Save, More and the backup
    disclosure (down), info, `</>`, sun and moon, ◀ ▶.
  - *Fonts* (§ 6.1): `--ov-font-script` for ar/fa (`"Segoe UI"`, Tahoma, `"SF Arabic"`, `"Geeza Pro"`, `"Noto Sans Arabic UI"`,
    `"Noto Sans Arabic"`, `"Noto Naskh Arabic UI"`) and he (`"Segoe UI"`, Arial, `"Arial Hebrew"`, `"Noto Sans Hebrew"`), placed
    in the token stacks after the Latin fonts and **before `system-ui` and the generic family**, as RT3 (d) requires (DejaVu Sans
    covers both scripts and would otherwise draw them); the canvas labels of SC11 read the same variable. Every hard-coded English
    `font-family` (30 selectors and the two SVG attributes) is repeated with the script fallback.
  - *Typography*: letter-spacing and upper case off for ar/fa/he (one `:root :is(…)` rule, as SC3); the seven italic sites upright;
    for ar/fa only, the English 9–11 px UI text at 12 px (24 selectors; SVG plot text, axis ticks and `code` keep their sizes until
    RT7/RT12 measure plot geometry) and the line-height floors of Hindi (1.5 on 9 control selectors, 1.7 on 64 text selectors),
    to be recalibrated by RT7 under Noto Sans Arabic. Hebrew keeps English's sizes and lines (Noto Sans Hebrew's line is 1.36 em).
  - *Deviation from the plan* (§ 2.3 put the Arabic additions into `scripts.css`'s selector lists): they are in `rtl.css`, so
    `scripts.css` and the structure `scripts.test.ts` asserts are untouched while SC10/SC12 edit them. `scripts.test.ts` now reads
    its CSS helpers from the new `src/theme/cssRules.ts` (shared with `rtl.test.ts`) and no longer counts `rtl.css` as English.
- **`src/theme/rtl.test.ts`** (source scan): no physical inline-axis declaration in any sheet without its `physical:` note, and
  exactly the seven listed; `rtl.css` imported once and scoped (no `:lang(en|es|pt|fr)`, no `:dir()`, no `!important`, no font
  loading); the island list, each island class existing in the sources, the antenna pad's areas; the three flips against their
  English rules; the stacks (DejaVu never named); and every English font-family, italic, sub-12 px size and low line-height with
  its line, by the same rules as `scripts.test.ts`.
- **RT6, placement.** `src/polish/popupPlacement.ts` (pure, `popupPlacement.test.ts`) holds the popup menu's arithmetic. Right to
  left, a form's popup menu opens from the pointer toward the inline end: its right edge at the pointer, the menu to its left, 4 px
  inside the viewport when there is no room; a keyboard-opened menu starts at the list's top-right corner. Left to right,
  `firstLeft`, `shiftX` and `keyboardAnchor` return exactly the values `PopupMenu.tsx` computed before (the test compares them with
  the old expressions). `placeTourCard` (`src/help/tourPlacement.ts`) takes the direction (`SiteTour.tsx` passes `uiDirection()`):
  right to left the card goes left of a target (side `left`), aligns with its right edge below or above it, and docks at the right
  on phones; `tourPlacement.test.ts` checks that every right-to-left placement is the mirror image of the left-to-right one and that
  the default is unchanged.
- **`tests/scripts/rtl-css.scripts.ts`** (under `npm run test:scripts`): (1) for `en`, `es-419`, `pt-BR` and `fr` at 320 and 1440 px,
  every element's box and its computed value of every property `rtl.css` declares are the same with and without the sheet on the
  home page and the 16 routes (SC3's method); (2) under `qps-rtl` at 1440 px: the sidebar at the right edge with the body's padding
  on the right and its border on the left, the page bar's place right of its tools and section right of page with the flipped
  separator, the More menu aligned with its button's left edge, a collapsed section's chevron at 90°, and the tour's sections step
  with its card left of the sidebar (side `left`) and Skip right of Next; (3) at 1440 and 320 px the hot list's popup menu with its
  right edge at the pointer (or 4 px inside), its items aligned at the start, and the keyboard menu at the list's top-right
  corner; (4) the spectrum's plot, sliders and ◀ ▶ buttons left to right with ◀ left of ▶ and a slider whose value grows to the
  right, the sky map's `svg` and `canvas` left to right with `ctx.direction` `inherit`, the antenna pad (`ltr`, North above
  South, East left of West where shown, its grid areas unchanged), and the optical and radio hot-list native rows in byte order on
  screen (the radio list loads `Pulsars.HTL`, whose rows start with the pulsar's ID).
  Chromium gives a keyboard-opened context menu (Shift+F10) a position inside the list, so there the menu opens as from a pointer;
  the corner anchor is for events without one and is checked in `popupPlacement.test.ts`. A native range given `direction:rtl`
  reverses in Chromium (Home → 0, then ArrowRight stays 0, a click near the right end gives 1 of 10); the test checks that a range
  in the right-to-left page behaves as in English (1 and 9) and that such a control reverses.

**LTR proof.**
- *Main against this branch (Chromium, one-off, not a spec).* `main` d5efd6c (a `git archive` copy with its own Vite cache) and this
  branch, each on its own dev server on loopback, loaded side by side for `en`, `es-419`, `pt-BR` and `fr` (the `?lang=fr` preview)
  at 320 and 1440 px on the home page and the 16 routes, the clock fixed and the tour marked seen, the header tools opened below
  600 px, each page snapshotted once two snapshots in a row agreed: for every element of `<body>` (and the root), **every** computed
  property `getComputedStyle` lists (547–548, sorted by name), its border box and the first line box of each of its text nodes.
  136 pages, 296,450 elements: **all identical**. One normalisation: a computed `text-align` of `start`/`end` in a left-to-right
  element is read as `left`/`right` (the keyword is what changed, not the alignment; main already computes `start` for 1,631–2,358
  elements per page as the initial value). Three pages needed a second load to settle. A deliberate change (the page bar's
  `padding-inline:12px 16px`) was reported on 24 elements at 1440 px, so the comparison sees a 4 px move. The `<head>`'s style tags
  are not compared (the branch has one more: `rtl.css`).
- *With and without `rtl.css`* (the spec above, kept in CI): the same for the four languages, both widths, every route.
- *Exports and render budget*: `exports-identity.spec.ts` and `render-budget.spec.ts` pass unchanged. `npm run build` passes with
  every check; `dist/` gains `rtl.css`'s rules in the main stylesheet and the RT6 helpers.

**Screenshots** (`qps-rtl`, not committed): `~/.cache/openvireo-rt56-shots/` holds the home page and the 16 routes at 320 and
1440 px, the More menu, the Help menu and About at both widths, and the tour's sections step at 1440 px. They show the sidebar on
the right, the page bar and its menus mirrored, VIREO's English About panel and plots left to right, ◀ left of ▶.

**Left for RT7 and later.** The RTL route walk (RT7) and its `KNOWN` list: at 320 px the phone header's brand, longer in `qps-rtl`
and `nowrap`, runs under the Tools button (a length problem, not a mirroring one); the Arabic sizes and line-height floors are the
plan's starting values, to be measured under Noto Sans Arabic; translated text inside islands (axis titles, the ◀ ▶ row's labels)
reads by the Unicode algorithm in a left-to-right paragraph until `plotText()` reaches the label sites (RT12); `dir` on the selector's
options and notices (RT9); per-input direction (RT11). `scripts.css` is unchanged.

**Verification.** `npx tsc -b`; Vitest `src/theme`, `src/polish`, `src/help`, `src/i18n` 1,057 in 40 files (`rtl.test.ts` 13,
`popupPlacement.test.ts` 2, `tourPlacement.test.ts` 3); the whole Vitest suite 5,681 with 2 load timeouts while the proof ran,
whose files pass alone (224 in 8 files); `npm run build`. Playwright, Chromium (dev server, DejaVu fontconfig): `page-bar`, `shell`,
`front-p-polish`, `onboarding`, `render-budget`, `exports-identity` 89 passed and 1 load flake (`front-p-polish` "Hot List loads the
bundled Pulsars.HTL", its response capture; passes alone); Firefox `page-bar` and `front-p-polish` 27. `npm run test:scripts` in two
shards: 59 + 37 passed (`rtl-css.scripts.ts` 11, about 30 s per language and width).

## RTL groundwork: the RTL walk (RT7)

Front RT7 (plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-rtl.md` § 5.2 and the RT7 row of § 9). Groundwork only: no right-to-left language is
registered, so everything here runs under the dev-only `qps-rtl` (`<html lang="ar" dir="rtl">`). **Nothing changes for a left-to-right
visitor** (proof below). The plan's RT14 (deep per-exercise walks under `qps-rtl`) is done here too: SC12's walks now run under
`qps-rtl` with every check.

- **The route walk** (`tests/scripts/rtl.scripts.ts`, scripts config, SC1/RT3 test fonts). At 320 and 1440 px: the home page and the
  16 routes (read from the `Route` type of `src/components/routePages.ts`), the header menu (the phone Tools panel at 320 px), the page
  bar's More menu, Help menu and Help › Websites, the options dialog (instructor mode), the hot list and its popup menu, OpenVireo's
  help viewer, VIREO's help topics and a topic, About, and the six tour steps (started from Help). After each step: `<html lang="ar"
  dir="rtl">`, the ⟦ marker, `layoutProblems()` and `verticalInkProblems()` (SC3's options), `islandProblems()`, `mirrorProblems()`,
  `ltrRunProblems()` (RT4) and, below 600 px, that the brand and the Tools button do not overlap; no page error. The language selector
  is not shown under a pseudo-locale, so its dialog is RT9's (with `dir` on its options).
- **The deep walks** (`tests/scripts/walks.scripts.ts`). The eleven SC12 walks run under `qps-rtl` at 320 **and** 1440 px with the
  whole `layoutProblems()` check (overflow, leaving the viewport, crossing a card or dialog, controls leaving their dialogs, plot text;
  SC12 asserted only "clipped" before RT5 mirrored the page) plus the three right-to-left checks after every step.
- **New checks** (`tests/fixtures/bidiProblems.ts`, beside RT4's `ltrRunProblems`):
  - `rtlIslandSelectors(css)` reads the island list from `src/theme/rtl.css` § 1 itself; `islandProblems()` requires every visible
    element it selects to compute `direction:ltr`, and every `[lang]` element below the root its language's direction (ar, fa, he,
    iw, ur right to left; any other left to right).
  - `mirrorProblems()`: the fixed sidebar (from 1100 px) ends at the right edge and the content is left of it; the page bar's place is
    right of its tools on a shared row; an open page-bar menu is inside the viewport and, from 600 px, starts at its button's left
    (inline-end) edge; a dialog's close button is nearer its left edge; the tour card is never on the right of its outline.
  - A test makes the page left to right again and requires each check to report what it exists for (sidebar, content, page bar,
    More menu, an island and a nested English element turned right to left, an Arabic element turned left to right).
- **`ltrRunProblems`, decided here** (RT4 left it to RT7): two right-to-left readings are correct and no longer reported. A number or
  date made only of digits, `/ . : , ،` and at least one RLM or ALM is a locale's own right-to-left format (ICU's Arabic date
  «10‏/11‏/2026، 10:48:21 م», which reads day first from the right; the site writes no RLM or ALM itself); an unsigned range of two numbers joined by an en dash ("3700–4700 Å", "1992–93") is drawn "4700–3700" and reads from 3700 to
  4700 right to left (plan § 3.4). Still problems: a signed range ("−1–1" is drawn "1–1−") and a hyphenated date. Measured in
  Chromium 156: "2009-06-01" inside an Arabic sentence is drawn "01-06-2009", and "2000-01-01T12:00:00" splits in the same way, so a
  date in a right-to-left sentence needs an isolate (a `{param}`).

**Results.** No `isolate` problem anywhere (every isolate the runtime writes reads left to right on every route and step of every walk),
no island, language-direction or mirror problem, no page error, and every text is marked. Fixed here (each LTR-neutral, proof below):
- *Print headers* (`FieldInfoListWindow.tsx:168`, `ClusterListWindow.tsx:20`, `ClusterDataWindow.tsx:244`, `VariableStarsRoute.tsx:199`):
  the screen date joined to translated text is wrapped in RT4's `iso()` (FSI…PDI in a right-to-left load, unchanged otherwise), so
  «10‏/11‏/2026، 10:48:21 م» stays one unit beside the laboratory line. The print *builders* (`clusterHrPrintPage`, `helpPrintPage`,
  `lightCurvePrintPage`, `pulsarPrintPage`, `infoListPrintPage`, `hotListPrintHeader`, `printSkyHeader`) take the date as `dateText` and
  compose it with VIREO's pieces; they are RT12's with the other composed sites below.
- *Plot titles*: the spectrum's rotated y-axis title (`SpectrumInstrument.tsx`) crossed its plot's left edge by 3 px at 320 px and the
  Multi-Spec spectrum's (`MultiSpecDisplay.tsx`, display and print) by 2–4 px under Noto Sans Arabic; both are wrapped in SC11's
  `keepInsideSvg` (`src/plotTextFit.ts`), which moves a title only when it crosses by more than half a pixel. This also settles SC12's
  tolerated `qps-cjk` entry for the Multi-Spec title (1 px at 1440 px), now removed from `KNOWN`.
- *Fixed text heights* (`src/theme/rtl.css` § 6): the classification cursor read-out (English's fixed 86 px) cut its third line by
  5 px at Arabic's 1.7 line height (E03, comparison marks, 320 px). As `scripts.css` § 6 does for ja, zh, hi and ru, it now has
  `height:auto;min-height:max(86px,calc(3lh + 26px))` for ar and fa, and the phone sidebar `min-height:60px`; `rtl.test.ts` pins both.
  Otherwise RT5's Arabic sizes and line-height floors (12 px, 1.5 on controls, 1.7 on text) cut no ink anywhere the walks go under Noto
  Sans Arabic, the worst-case face (RT3): they are kept as measured values, not starting values.

**`KNOWN`** (`tests/fixtures/rtlKnown.ts` names each site's problem, file, owner and reason once; the two specs list where each is
expected, one entry per walk, width set and exact step, every one required except the font-dependent ones; E02's "(.TXT)" on the
results button is one entry required at either "LSS lab guide" or "LSS survey", as the lazily loaded survey renders behind the lab
guide or only once it opens. `knownProblem()` (RT7 review) never accepts an isolate the bidi runtime wrote, whatever a site's
pattern, and accepts a `catalogLiteral` run only when the run is written literally in `src` (`.ts`, `.tsx`, `.json`, tests left out),
so a value the runtime formatted or composed outside an isolate is never taken for a catalog literal):
- **RT8, catalog text** (`catalogLiteral`): every Latin run the walks found inside a translated sentence is a literal of the English
  catalog or a map leaf that `qps-rtl` keeps in Latin, as a translation would — signed numbers and limits ("−21.4", "−90", "+54",
  "-2,400,000"), dates ("2009-06-01", "1900-01-01", "11-24-2008", "2000-01-01T12:00:00"), signed ranges ("−1–1"), formulas
  ("10^((m−M)/5"), "8-bit", "0332+5434", and Latin tokens before an ellipsis ("ID...", "Date/Time...", "Site...", "PLR…", "band…",
  "Isochrones…", "Exercise..."; RT4's "parenthesised English in a translation"). RT7's decision: the fix is in the catalog, not the walk
  — a value becomes a `{param}` (which `t()` isolates) and RT8's right-to-left lint flags a literal date, signed number, signed range or
  a Latin token before `…`/`...` typed into an `ar`, `fa` or `he` catalog. A runtime rule that isolates parenthesised Latin groups in
  `t()` was considered and left to RT8: it would also need the maps and `vireo()` translations, and the lint has to exist anyway.
- **RT8 and RT12, a sign before a parameter** (`signBeforeParam`, RT7 review): the radio B1950 note's "−89.427" is not a catalog
  literal — `RadioAntennaMessages.ts` writes "−{threshold}°", and its translator (like those of `PulsarMessages.ts`,
  `PulsarLoadSaveMessages.ts`, `PulsarSavedRowsMessages.ts`, `RadioFinishSaveMessages.ts` and `RadioRecordSaveMessages.ts`)
  substitutes `{params}` without the FSI…PDI `t()` adds. Isolating the parameter alone would still draw "89.427−", so the sign belongs
  in the parameter (RT8's lint: a sign before `{…}`), and each of those substitution points needs `iso()` (RT12).
- **RT12, data, numbers and composed text rendered straight into elements** (plan § 3.2's per-site list): the spectrum's z read-out and
  ×1/×10 step factors (`SpectrumInstrument.tsx`), the draws "— / 765" (`StarExposure.tsx`), " (.TXT)" appended to a translated button
  (`LssSurvey.tsx`), the wedge details, raw line and table cells (`WedgeLab.tsx`), the inspected DAT record's fields and bit forms
  (`NativeObservationResults.tsx`), the Pleiades fit percentage (`PleiadesAnalysis.tsx`), the Mayall domain table
  (`PhotometerDomainExplanation.tsx`), the trial-fit table and VIREO's model name (`NativeClusterAnalysis.tsx`), a possible member's
  declination (`PossibleMemberWindow.tsx`), the "n / total" list status (`FieldInfoListWindow.tsx:114`), the plotted pairs
  (`ClusterNativeObservationHr.tsx`), the GCVS results' declinations and type codes such as "EA/D:" (`VariableStarsRoute.tsx`, 106 cells
  on the route), the saved-rows identity sentence (`PulsarSavedRows.tsx`), the pointing's declination (`OpenVireo.tsx`), Object X's
  measured coordinates (`ObjectXMeasure.tsx`), the site clock (`SiteClock.tsx`) and Site Information's lines, a translated label and a
  coordinate in one LTR island (`SiteDialogs.tsx`). Plot text whose box (not ink) crosses its plot because Noto Sans Arabic is the
  primary face of the ar stack: VIREO's native "(B-V)" frame label (`core/clusterNativeObservationHr.ts`, 5 px below), and, tolerated
  as font-dependent (≤2 px), Object X's end wavelengths (`ObjectXSpectrum.tsx`) and the pulsar recording's name (`PulsarAnalysis.tsx`);
  the Multi-Spec selection number 3 reaches 4–6 px above its field (`core/overlayLabels.ts`). Also RT12's, not measurable by this walk:
  `plotText()` at the translated SVG labels (23 `<text>{t(…)|vireo(…)}` sites in 14 files by a source scan; about 40 with the computed
  ones, plan § 0.1) — in an LTR svg an Arabic title draws as one right-to-left run, which `ltrRunProblems` does not check — and `ltrText()`
  at the 39 call sites that pass `vireo` to a builder (hot-list miss and added texts, help and pulsar print pages, the LSS region
  summary, the cluster empty-field text, receiver captions and marks, Multi-Spec information and print lines, the variable-star
  confirmations…): under View original their English pieces are isolated one by one.
- **RT13, help** (`helpRows`, `src/clusters/CleaHelp.tsx`, `src/help/SpectralLineTable.tsx`): VIREO's English help rows (topic lists,
  topic text, the Spectral Line Table and its help) carry no `lang="en"` while no translated help exists for the page language, so
  they run right to left ("-Telescope Controls-", a trailing period at the left).
- **RT13 too**: the Exit Observatory reminder (VIREO's English `Reminder.obs`, `src/help/HelpMenus.tsx`). Its text is fetched and
  E08's radio route is lazily loaded, so the observatory and help walks wait for the reminder's text and E08's "radio card" step for
  the radio route before they are checked (RT7 review); every entry is then required.
- **RT14, long words at 320 px** (shared with the Russian front's SC12 entries, now `qps-cyrl` and `qps-rtl`): E06's receiver
  properties table and the radio Record Data properties box.
- **Pseudo-locale artifacts** (kept so any other problem at those steps still fails): the brand over the Tools button at 320 px
  (every translation keeps "OpenVireo"; `qps-rtl` writes 11 Arabic letters; `qps-cyrl` overlaps it too, by 31 px, and no walk checks
  that); and three overflows at 320 px (E02's wedge status, the help topic dialogs' note, the light-curve print preview's note, the last
  at both widths) from `pseudoMark`'s unbreakable "·" padding of a map function's English result, which Noto Sans Arabic draws wider
  than DejaVu.

**LTR proof.**
- The TSX changes are LTR-neutral by construction: `iso()` returns `String(value)` in a left-to-right load (RT4's string identity test
  covers it), and `keepInsideSvg` leaves a title that fits untransformed; a new test loads `#spectrum` in en, es-419, pt-BR and fr and
  the Multi-Spec display and print preview in English and in every language whose observatory is translated (`translatedFor
  ('observatory')`: es-419 and pt-BR, and fr once FR1 is merged; RT7 review) at 320 and 1440 px and requires both titles' wrappers to
  carry no transform.
- `rtl.css` § 6 is scoped by `:lang(ar)`/`:lang(fa)`: `rtl-css.scripts.ts`'s computed-style equality (every element's box and every
  property the sheet declares, with and without the sheet, en/es-419/pt-BR/fr, 320 and 1440 px, home and the 16 routes) passes.
- `exports-identity.spec.ts` (English, Spanish, Portuguese and `qps-rtl` variants), `shell.spec.ts` and `page-bar.spec.ts` pass.

**Main was red before this front.** CI run 42997287745 (main fdec3f6, RT5/RT6) failed the scripts part 2: SC12's E03 walk under
`qps-rtl` at 320 px asserts ink, and RT5's 1.7 line height cut the cursor read-out's third line by 5.2 px — the § 6 fix above. Main
took the read-out rule as a hotfix (f404879); the merge keeps one copy of it, with this front's sidebar rule.

**CI.** The `scripts` job is now three parts (`.github/workflows/ci-cd.yml`, still one job id, so `deploy` keeps `needs: [unit, e2e,
pages, scripts]`): part 2 is the deep walks under `qps-deva`, `qps-cyrl` and `qps-rtl` (66 tests, `--grep="under qps-(deva|cyrl|rtl) at "`),
part 3 the two right-to-left files `rtl-css.scripts.ts` and `rtl.scripts.ts` (17, matched by file name), part 1 the complement of both
(107: fonts, routes, help-unicode, bidi and the `qps-cjk` walks). `--list` confirms the three selections are disjoint and cover all 190
tests. Locally (2 workers, this machine) the parts took 6.8, 8.3 and 3.0 min (round 2: 6.2, 8.3 and 3.0 min); main's last run took 14.2 min for the
old part 1 job and 8.3 for part 2, so each new part should stay well under 15 min in CI.

**Verification.** `npx tsc -b`; Vitest `src/i18n`, `src/theme` and `src/components` 853 in 35 files (`rtl.test.ts` 14); `npm run build`
with every check (2169 files, deploy limits). Playwright, Chromium: the three scripts parts as above, twice (190 of 190 each round);
`tests/i18n/shell.spec.ts` and `tests/page-bar.spec.ts` 47 of 47; `exports-identity.spec.ts` `[qps-rtl]` and `[pt-BR]` 38 of 38 (DejaVu
fontconfig, dev server).

### RT7 independent review

Reviewer: Claude Opus 5.5 at high effort, a separate subagent that did not write the front (2026-11-11), on the branch merged
with main's cursor read-out hotfix (f404879).

**Raw findings.**
1. *Medium.* `catalogLiteral` (`/^bidi run: “.*” in (?!pre\.)/`) accepted any Latin run at its listed steps, so a value the runtime
   formatted outside an isolate there would have passed as a catalog literal. Checked against `src`, one of the runs it accepted is
   not a literal: the radio B1950 note's "−89.427" is a catalog sign before a `{threshold}` that `createRadioAntennaTranslator`
   substitutes without an isolate (five other message translators substitute the same way).
2. *Low.* "An isolate the runtime writes is never KNOWN" held only because no pattern began with "bidi isolate"; nothing enforced it.
3. *Low.* The RLM/ALM acceptance covered any run holding RLM or ALM, letters and hyphens included; the site writes neither, so only
   ICU's numeric formats need it.
4. *Low.* The two tolerated (*unstable*) entries were timing-dependent only because the walks checked before the work finished (the
   fetched Exit reminder, E08's lazily loaded radio route); E02's "(.TXT)" was attributed to "LSS lab guide" but is first seen at
   "LSS survey" when the survey chunk arrives late (the E02 qps-rtl walk failed at 320 px with four workers).
5. *Low.* The LTR proof for the Multi-Spec y-axis title covered English only, while es-419 and pt-BR translate VIREO's title.
6. Checked and correct: the four print-header `iso()` calls and both `keepInsideSvg` wrappers are identity / untransformed in a
   left-to-right load; `rtl.css` § 6 is scoped by `:lang`; the three CI selections are disjoint and cover all 190 tests (`--list`),
   the deep walks under every pseudo-locale run (qps-cjk in part 1, the others in part 2), and `deploy` still needs `scripts`.

**Resolution.** (1, 2) `tests/fixtures/rtlKnown.ts` `knownProblem()`, used by both specs: a "bidi isolate" problem is never known, and
a `literal` site (`catalogLiteral`) accepts a run only when it is written in `src`; the B1950 run is its own required site
`signBeforeParam` (RT8 for the sign, RT12 for the translators' missing `iso()`); a self-test pins both rules. (3) The exemption is
`^(?=.*[RLM ALM])[digits RLM ALM / . : , ،]+$`, with a self-test that an RLM beside a hyphenated date is still a problem. (4) The
observatory and help walks wait for the reminder's text and E08's radio card step for the radio route; *unstable* is gone and every
entry is required; E02's lab-guide entry names either step (`/^LSS (?:lab guide|survey)$/`). (5) The LTR test walks the Multi-Spec
display and print in every language of `translatedFor('observatory')`, each in its own context, and requires that every plot was
seen.

**Review verification** (merged branch, this machine, the workflow's commands with `--workers=2`): `npx tsc -b`; Vitest `src/i18n`,
`src/theme`, `src/components` 853 in 35 files; `npm run build` with every check (2169 files). Scripts part 1 107 of 107 (9.2 min),
part 2 66 of 66 (9.1–12.7 min; its first run caught the B1950 step's `catalogLiteral` entry, which only the misclassified run had
matched, and passed after the entry was dropped), part 3 17 of 17 (4.3 min). Playwright (DejaVu fontconfig, dev server):
`tests/i18n/shell.spec.ts`, `tests/page-bar.spec.ts`, `tests/observatory.spec.ts`, `tests/i18n/observatory.spec.ts`,
`tests/i18n/e08.spec.ts` and `tests/i18n/help.spec.ts` (the walks the review edited) 107 of 107.


## French: the observatory and VIREO's names (FR1)

The second French front, plan `analysis/front-plans/front-i18n-fr.md` §6.2 "FR1" in the research root, with PT1 as its model.
French stays hidden (`released:false`): only `?lang=fr` reaches it, and fr and fr-* browsers still open English. The translations
are web adaptations by OpenVireo, never CLEA's; every file the site writes stays English.

**What is translated.**
- **Catalog:** PT1's 553 observatory keys (the instruments' prefixes `sky`, `optical`, `motion`, `horizon`, `siteClock`,
  `runningClock`, `scopeAccess`, `instrumentAccess`, `instrumentParameters`, `cameraParameters`, `cameraInfo`, `cameraPrepare`,
  `palette`, `testMode`, `optionsFile`, `photometerTab`, `htl`, `multispec`, `nativeMirror`, `hubblePoint`, the hot list's Hubble
  reasons, and the 104 guide keys the observatory files render: `classLab`, `pleiadesLab`, `pleiadesPoint`, `ph`, `continuity`…;
  about 8,150 English words), plus the welcome's 26 `home.*` keys (Front HOME landed after FR0, and the observatory walk starts on
  the welcome). `fr.json` now holds 709 keys. `fr.test.ts` pins the prefix set, the 104 guide keys (`FR1_GUIDE_KEYS`) and `home.*`.
- **Maps, complete:** `observatory`, `fieldInfo`, `fieldReadDetails`, `nakedEye`, `qsoList`, `sound`, `observationMetadata`, `camera`
  (the observatory's CCD/IR card, as PT1) and `polish` (partial since FR0).
- **All of VIREO's originals at once:** the 762 distinct texts Portuguese covers (Spanish's 748 originals and 14 identical, plus
  `General` and `E`). 736 are in `src/i18n/fr/originals.json`; 26 equal the English in French and carry an `identical.json` reason
  instead (OK, Options, Options..., Confirmation, Minutes, Min, Altitude, Horizon, Longitude, Latitude, Zoom, Style, Minimum,
  Maximum, Mode, Type, Site..., Pulsar, Radio, Radio..., - Page 2, HH:MM:SS, V - Mv =, NVO, Project CLEA, and the longitude letter
  E, Est). FR0's two kept names (NVO, Project CLEA) moved from `originals.json` to `identical.json` only, so a kept original is
  never also translated (a test). `General` is «Général» (`FR_ONLY_ORIGINALS`, as `PT_ONLY_ORIGINALS`); a label with a colon is
  never identical («Longitude :», U+202F before the colon).
- **Fragments** keep VIREO's join spaces («Le fichier », « ne contient pas de données de décalage vers le rouge LSS !»);
  `fragment|Meter Radio Telescope` is «Radiotélescope de {diameter} m» (a `placeholders` allowance, as es and pt). The saved
  observations file pair, whose two pieces are both ours, uses « … » (`Fichier d’observations enregistré « ` and ` » chargé.`), and
  the overwrite question quotes « Non » and « Annuler ». The two pieces VIREO joins with an untranslated straight `"` keep it
  (`" Not Found.` → `" introuvable.`, the photometer's readings question), each with a `quotes` allowance, as in Portuguese.

**How it was translated.** Ten Sonnet 5.5 batches (`model: "sonnet"`), from the English with the reviewed Spanish and Portuguese
as meaning context only, under one brief (plan §3–§5: vous, infinitive controls, vous-imperative instructions, sentence case,
accented capitals, ordinary spaces before ; : ! ? and inside « » for the fixer, ’, «décalage vers le rouge», «raie», «pose»,
the glossary's VIREO names, the English gloss at a VIREO name's first mention in web prose): three catalog batches (185, 185 and
183 keys, merged with `scripts/i18n-batch.mjs merge --lang fr`), three originals batches (251, 251, 250), and four map batches
(observatory; camera; the six small maps; polish). Then the fixer's fix mode, then the strict lint. The Opus implementer read
every row and changed 86 strings beyond typography (43 catalog, 13 originals, 30 map strings), and wrote the 26 `home.*` keys:
- **One form per VIREO name.** VIREO's Telescope Display is «affichage du télescope» in the Multi-Spec keys (5), as its original
  («Cliquez sur des objets dans l’affichage du télescope…»), while the finder/telescope view switch stays «vue du télescope»; Save
  All is «Tout enregistrer» everywhere (2 web notes wrote «Enregistrer tout»); the hot-list editor is «Listes d’objets d’origine»
  (3 strings wrote «originales»); the Multi-Object Spectrometer «spectromètre multiobjet» (2); «l’Objet X», never «objet X» or
  «Object X» (8); the 0.4m telescope «télescope de 0.4m» (2).
- **VIREO messages quoted in French**, as their originals read: «Aucune étoile trouvée dans le champ !», «Le champ contient trop de
  régions !», «Toutes les variables du GCVS dans la région.», «(non consigné)» (3), «(Image de l’écran imprimée sur la page
  suivante)», «Inconnu», «Numéro de table de laboratoire (non consigné)».
- **Instructor notes:** «Effet immédiat ici (session de la page ; Quitter ne le change pas…)» for every live option note (7 wrote
  «En direct ici» or «Actif ici»); the camera's bias «biais» and A/D «A/N» as the original «Profondeur A/N (bits)» (4).
- **Guide steps** in the infinitive like the others («Pointer une étoile de la liste», «Recopier vos lectures», «Constituer
  l’échantillon», «Conserver votre investigation»); «puis choisissez Calculer B−V» (2); the standards a practice lies between are
  «deux étalons voisins»; «Un type ou une justification non consignés bloquent le changement».
- **Originals:** «Êtes-vous sûr ?» (was «Confirmer ?»); «(40 caractères max.)», «(12 caractères max.)», «sur 4 chiffres» and
  «(UT − heure locale)» (ASCII brackets that read as English glosses); «Définir E(B-V) à la valeur correcte…» (the glossary name);
  the radio receivers' «(secondaire)» and «récepteur principal» (was esclave/maître, as PT1's «secundário»/«principal»);
  «Informations» for VIREO's Information; «Sec.» for Secs/Sec (with «Sec» the View original walk could not tell the French from
  VIREO's English; «Degrés» for Degs/Deg since the review); «Commentaires / notes» (the slash-joined word overflowed the 320 px
  entry form).
- **Lint findings fixed in the text:** glosses the English does not hold (`(Hot List)`, `(Request Time)`, «(Guide Star Catalog,
  GSC)»), a gloss with the forbidden «Redshift», three glyph counts (– › …), «tracking» as «suivi», an elision before a VIREO name
  («lorsque l’option Afficher…»), «le bouton OK de VIREO».
The batches' own notes (uncertain «poste» for "bench", «voie» for "route", «S/B» for S/N, «trame» for raster, «Respecter la casse»)
were read and kept.

**The VIREO menu glossary (fr).** FR0's names were reviewed as they reached the screen; `glossary.json` `names` are now 201. Added:
Save All › Tout enregistrer, Clear All › Tout effacer, Telescope Display › affichage du télescope, Telescope view › vue du
télescope, Original hot lists › Listes d’objets d’origine, Master Receiver › récepteur principal, Case Dependent › Respecter la
casse, Search String › Texte à rechercher, Search Results › Résultats de la recherche, Optical › Optique (exact), Spectral Line
Help › Aide sur les raies spectrales. **Loose stems tightened** (FR0's list): exposure › «pose», load › «charg», count ›
«compt|nombre», slew › «d[ée]plac», seeing › «seeing», merge › «fusion», bin › «canal|canaux»; every French string passes them.
turn-off stays loose for FR4 (plan §5.1). Six `lint-allowed.json` entries, each with its reason: the radio caption's `{diameter}`,
the two straight-quote pieces, the Two Micron All Sky Survey title-case run, and the multiplication dot of «c·z» (twice), which
the point médian rule would otherwise read as inclusive writing.
- **Names later fronts must keep:** FR1's guide keys quote E03 and E04 controls that FR3 and FR4 translate: Vérifier la
  classification, Enregistrer la classification, Réessayer le chargement de la source, Objet d’entraînement, Source de
  classification, Bibliothèque d’entraînement d’origine, Étalon − entraînement, Enregistrer/Restaurer l’investigation complète;
  Activer le photomètre des Pléiades, Cible de photométrie, Pointage réel du télescope, Télescope prêt, Filtre du photomètre,
  Position du ciel, Calculer B−V, Démarrer l’intégration, Ajouter une entrée au carnet, Analyse couleur–magnitude de l’exercice.
  Those fronts use these forms or update the guide keys with them.

**`vireoSites` strict for fr.** `src/i18n/vireoSites.test.ts` `STRICT` gains French; `UNRESOLVED_SITES` is still 140 (rerun).

**Tests.**
- `src/i18n/fr/fr.test.ts`: the observatory keys, guide keys and welcome present; the nine maps complete; every VIREO original
  translated or kept with a reason, never both (762, with `General` and `E`); the fixer a no-op; the fr lint over the 709 keys, 16
  maps and 736 originals (no errors; length reports for the reviewer, mostly VIREO captions such as «Afficher l’identifiant de
  l’objet» for Show Object ID, 2.36).
- `tests/i18n/languageText.ts`: `FRENCH_TEXT.done` is `{shell, observatory}` and its `observatoryMaps` the 16 French maps, so
  `observatory.spec.ts` runs its nine language checks in French under their own titles. `walks/observatory.ts`: `SAME_IN.fr` (the
  identical words above, «Gain (e⁻/ADU)», CCD, IR, Multi-Spec, Pulsars…; not Deg or Sec, which French translates) and
  `NOT_SAME_IN.fr` (General).
- `tests/i18n/exports-identity.spec.ts`: the hot list, options file (`VEO_OPTS.json`) and Field Information (Star Data List Save
  Text and Comma Delimit, QSO List Save Text) families gain an `[fr]` variant: same names and SHA-256 as English, no French letter,
  « » or U+202F in the files. `N()` takes French through the branch Portuguese uses (its catalog text, the ` ↗` suffix).
- `tests/i18n/areas.spec.ts`: FR0's partial-language case now expects some empty areas (`> 0`, was `> 5`): FR1's maps and keys also
  give text to `classification` (the observationMetadata map) and `fits` (`cameraInfo.*`), so five areas stay empty (cluster,
  pulsar, radio, lss, exposure) and each still fetches no chunk.
- `exports-identity.spec.ts` `N()`: a language before its release falls back to the English instead of throwing, so its “has a
  translation” check now also requires the text to differ from the English (otherwise `Open dome ↗` was looked up without its
  arrow split). Released languages behave as before.

**Review** (independent, 2026-11-11). Every French string FR1 adds was read (the 579 catalog keys it adds or changes, the nine maps, 736 originals).
Quality is high: vous throughout, U+202F before ; : ! ? and inside « », typographic apostrophes, sentence case, the IAU terms and
