the glossary's VIREO names (Enregistrer/Consigner, Localiser/Rechercher, Déplacer) hold across originals, catalog and maps, and no
string claims to be CLEA's. Findings:
1. *French errors (medium; fixed, 23 strings).* «entrées d’Tout enregistrer» (elision before a command name, now «de Tout
   enregistrer»); «c’est cet affichage … qui est imprimée» (agreement, «imprimé»); «l’option … est activé» (camera, «activée»);
   «100%» (now «100 %», as the page's own percent format writes it); «10000 ms» (now «10 000 ms», the thousands rule); the
   Multi-Spec fragment « ?» after the selection number had an ordinary space (now U+202F, joined as VIREO joins it); «(2MASS;
   Skrutskie…)» (U+202F before the semicolon); «Voulez-vous terminer la pose en cours ?» (now «interrompre», as Terminate Current
   Slew); one form for the redshift letter: the plan's «Consigner z» and «z (croissant)» against «(Z)», «Plage en Z», «Mesure de
   Z» (5 originals now «z», the IAU notation); «Mode édition» capitalised as VIREO's option name in the observatory map (6, as
   everywhere else); «Deg.» (not a French abbreviation; now «Degrés», as Spanish «Grados» and Portuguese «Graus», and «Déc.
   degrés :»); the cluster dialog's «s :» now «Sec. :», VIREO's Sec in one form. «Sec.» (a truncation, with its point) beside
   «Min» (the unit symbol) is correct French and kept; «Commentaires / notes» is kept for its 320 px reason.
2. *`SAME_IN.fr` (low; fixed).* It held Deg and Sec, which French translates, so an English Deg or Sec left on the observatory
   would have passed the walk; removed.
3. *`N()` fallback (medium; tightened).* With French before its release, `t.text` returns the English for a missing key, so an
   untranslated name in an `[fr]` family would have matched the page's English and passed. `N()` now throws for a language before
   its release unless its catalog has the key (an identical entry passes), as for a released language; Spanish and Portuguese
   are unchanged (es keeps its own branch; pt is released and throws as before).
4. *Areas guard (low; tightened).* `> 0` empty areas alone no longer said which areas carry French text. The test now pins them
   (`classification`, `fits`, `photometer`; each FR front updates the list) besides requiring an empty area, and each route's
   chunk set is still derived from the plan.
The 26 identical originals are each justified (the same word or symbol in French, a format, an acronym, an official name). On
`dist/`: index.html, sitemap, 404 and robots are unchanged against main 2daf61e (hashes normalised), no `/fr/` page exists, every
es and pt chunk is byte-identical, and the entry differs only in the fr core's preload list.

**Sizes** (`npm run build`, `check-i18n-dist.mjs`; compared with main 2daf61e built the same way, chunk hashes normalised). fr: 4
chunks, 66.8 KB gzip — core 63.3 KB gzip (208.7 KB raw; 8.3 KB in FR0), photometer 1.8 KB, fits 1.2 KB, classification 0.5 KB;
five areas still empty. Spanish and Portuguese: every chunk byte-identical (same names). English: every file identical but for
chunk hashes, except the entry `index.js`, +4 bytes: the fr core's preload list gains the `observatoryMenuLiterals` chunks it now
imports (as Spanish's and Portuguese's do); two same-named shared chunk pairs only swap their hash order. Deploy limits: 2,170
files.

**Verification** (2026-11-11, worktree `claude/front-fr1` from main 2daf61e). `npx tsc -b`; Vitest over `src/i18n`, `src/help`,
`src/seo` and `src/components`: 1,100 tests in 43 files; the pins refreshed (only `dynamic-key-sites.json`, two fr map heads like
Portuguese's); `npm run build` with every check. Playwright (dev server, Chromium, DejaVu fontconfig, two workers):
`observatory`, `shell`, `areas` and `language-selector` 95 (two failures in the first run: the areas guard above, fixed, and
the Spanish observatory walk's timeout, a load flake that passed when rerun with the other changed tests, 41 passed); the
exports-identity `[fr]` variants 4 (hot list, options file, Field Information, toolbar); `npm run test:scripts` in its two CI parts,
55 and 107; `npm run test:pages` on `dist/` 77. On `dist/` served from loopback (vite preview), fr-FR, fr-CA and fr browsers
without `?lang=` opened English with three options and fetched no French chunk; `?lang=fr` (en-US and fr-FR browsers) opened
French, `<html lang="fr">`, with only the fr core fetched on the observatory and four options. The 320 px screenshots of the
